  • 2017 年和 2018 年分离的病毒的 PB1 和 PB2 片段属于 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 谱系的不同簇,表明可能存在额外的重配事件。猪分离株形成一个离散簇,与 2007-2024 年注册的人类季节性 甲型H3N2流感病毒 菌株相距 ≥ 4 个抗原单位。

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Archives of Virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-31  |  DOI: 10.1007/s00705-026-06779-y  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 171, 12, 314
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

2017-2018 年在俄罗斯养猪场检测到的 甲型H3N2流感病毒 流感病毒的特征。

Characterization of influenza A(H3N2) viruses detected on a pig farm in Russia in 2017-2018.
作者： Alexey V. Danilenko, Svetlana V. Svyatchenko, Maria K. Bragina, Natalia I. Goncharova, Natalia P. Kolosova, Ksenia I. Ivanova, Andrei S. Gudymo, Natalia V. Danilchenko, Olga N. Perfilieva, Anna V. Maksyutova
摘要中文翻译猪充当甲型流感病毒的“混合容器”,促进种间 传播 和基因组重配。在此,我们报告了 2017 年 8 月和 2018 年 5 月从俄罗斯伊尔库茨克地区一家商业养猪场的哺乳仔猪和断奶仔猪中分离出的流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 病毒的特征。全 基因组 测序显示,它们的 HA 和 NA 片段与 2002-2004 年流行的人类季节性 甲型H3N2流感病毒 毒株聚集在一起,而内部基因盒源自甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 病毒。2017 年和 2018 年分离的病毒的 PB1 和 PB2 片段属于 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 谱系的不同簇,表明可能存在额外的重配事件。血凝抑制测定和抗原制图显示显着的 抗原漂移,猪分离株形成一个离散簇,与 2007-2024 年注册的人类季节性 甲型H3N2流感病毒 菌株相距 ≥ 4 个抗原单位。这些病毒积累了与猪适应相关的关键替换,包括HA1-A138S,但保留了对奥司他韦和扎那米韦的完全敏感性。在同一农场反复检测到密切相关的重配体表明它们在农场内部或外部持续循环,从而导致重新引入。需要进一步的研究来了解这一观察结果的潜在机制。因此,我们的研究在俄罗斯猪体内发现了人源流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 病毒的新遗传谱系,并再次强调,很久以前就停止在人群中传播的 季节性流感 病毒可能在猪和/或其他未知的中间体 宿主 中多年未被检测到,经历独立的 进化 并产生可能逃避预先存在的人类免疫的抗原性独特变体。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在表征在猪中检测到的流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 病毒,强调猪作为甲型流感病毒混合容器的作用以及种间 传播 和基因组重配的潜力。
设计与对象
该研究于 2017 年 8 月和 2018 年 5 月期间从俄罗斯伊尔库茨克地区的一个商业养猪场的哺乳仔猪和断奶仔猪中分离出流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 病毒。
核心方法
采用全基因组测序来分析HA和NA片段,它们与2002-2004年人类季节性甲型H3N2流感病毒毒株聚类,而内部基因盒被发现源自甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09病毒。还采用血凝抑制测定和抗原制图来评估 抗原漂移。
主要结果
2017 年和 2018 年分离的病毒的 PB1 和 PB2 片段属于 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 谱系的不同簇,表明可能存在额外的重配事件。猪分离株形成一个离散簇,与 2007-2024 年注册的人类季节性 甲型H3N2流感病毒 菌株相距 ≥ 4 个抗原单位。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,季节性流感 病毒可以在猪群中持续存在多年,经历独立的 进化 并产生可能逃避预先存在的人类免疫力的抗原性不同的变体。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了监测猪群中流感病毒的重要性,因为它们可能作为 储存宿主 来检测可能在人类群体中重新出现的抗原性不同的变异。
局限与证据强度
该研究指出,需要进一步的研究来了解观察到的重配事件的潜在机制,这表明对研究结果的解释有一定的信心。

Characterization of influenza A(H3N2) viruses detected on a pig farm in Russia in 2017-2018.

Authors: Alexey V. Danilenko, Svetlana V. Svyatchenko, Maria K. Bragina, Natalia I. Goncharova, Natalia P. Kolosova, Ksenia I. Ivanova, Andrei S. Gudymo, Natalia V. Danilchenko, Olga N. Perfilieva, Anna V. Maksyutova
Original abstract (English)
Pigs serve as "mixing vessels" for influenza A viruses, facilitating interspecies transmission and genomic reassortment. Here, we report characterization of influenza A(H3N2) viruses isolated from suckling and weaned piglets at one commercial pig farm in the Irkutsk Region, Russia, in August 2017 and May 2018. Full-genome sequencing revealed that their HA and NA segments clustered with human seasonal A(H3N2) strains circulating in 2002-2004, while the internal gene cassette was derived from A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses. PB1 and PB2 segments of viruses isolated in 2017 and 2018 belonged to different clusters of the A(H1N1)pdm09 lineage suggesting possible additional reassortment event. Hemagglutination inhibition assay and antigenic cartography demonstrated significant antigenic drift, with the swine isolates forming a discrete cluster ≥ 4 antigenic units distant from human seasonal A(H3N2) strains registered in 2007-2024. The viruses accumulated key substitutions, including HA1-A138S, associated with swine adaptation, yet retained full susceptibility to oseltamivir and zanamivir. Repeated detection of closely related reassortants at the same farm suggests their sustained circulation either within the farm or outside it, leading to reintroductions. Further studies are needed to understand the underlying mechanisms of this observation. Thus, our study identified a new genetic lineage of human-origin influenza A(H3N2) viruses in swine in Russia and highlighted once again that seasonal influenza viruses that stopped circulating in the human population long ago might remain undetected for years within swine and/or other unknown intermediate hosts, undergoing independent evolution and generating antigenically distinct variants that may evade pre-existing human immunity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to characterize influenza A(H3N2) viruses detected in pigs, highlighting the role of pigs as mixing vessels for influenza A viruses and the potential for interspecies transmission and genomic reassortment.
Design and population
The study involved isolating influenza A(H3N2) viruses from suckling and weaned piglets at a commercial pig farm in the Irkutsk Region, Russia, during August 2017 and May 2018.
Core methods
Full-genome sequencing was used to analyze the HA and NA segments, which clustered with human seasonal A(H3N2) strains from 2002-2004, while the internal gene cassette was found to be derived from A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses. Hemagglutination inhibition assay and antigenic cartography were also employed to assess antigenic drift.
Main results
The PB1 and PB2 segments of the viruses isolated in 2017 and 2018 belonged to different clusters of the A(H1N1)pdm09 lineage, suggesting a possible additional reassortment event. The swine isolates formed a discrete cluster ≥ 4 antigenic units distant from human seasonal A(H3N2) strains registered in 2007-2024.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that seasonal influenza viruses can persist in swine populations for years, undergoing independent evolution and generating antigenically distinct variants that may evade pre-existing human immunity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the importance of monitoring swine populations for influenza viruses, as they may serve as reservoirs for antigenically distinct variants that could potentially re-emerge in human populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study notes that further research is needed to understand the underlying mechanisms of the observed reassortment events, indicating moderate confidence in the interpretation of the findings.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

2025/26 年赛季末流感 疫苗 对中国北京 甲型H3N2流感病毒 和 B/Victoria 的有效性。

End-of-season influenza vaccine effectiveness against a(H3N2) and B/Victoria in Beijing, China, 2025/26 season.
作者： Jiaojiao Zhang, Ying Shen, Weixian Shi, Jiaxin Ma, Jiachen Zhao, Chunna Ma, Dan Wu, Yingying Wang, Guilan Lu, Lu Zhang
摘要中文翻译简介:本研究旨在评估中国北京 2025/26 季节末流感 疫苗 对 甲型H3N2流感病毒 和 B/Victoria 的有效性 (VE)。方法:我们采用测试阴性设计来评估2025年9月(第40/2025周)至2026年5月(第21/2026周)通过北京流感哨点病毒学监测系统入组的门诊流感样疾病(ILI)患者的流感VE。病例是流感检测呈阳性的ILI患者;对照组是那些测试结果呈阴性的人。使用非条件多元逻辑回归模型估计 VE,调整年龄(建模为自然三次样条)、性别、合并症的存在、居住区域和日历周(建模为自然三次样条)。结果:在 20,749 名接受检测的 ILI 患者中,4875 名(23.5%)流感呈阳性,其中 甲型H3N2流感病毒 和 B/Victoria 是主要毒株。针对任何流感的调整后 VE 为 26.4%(95% CI:17.8 至 34.1),针对 甲型H3N2流感病毒 为 22.2%(95% CI:11.3 至 31.7),针对 B/Victoria 为 34.7%(95% CI:19.6 至 47.0),其中在 18-59 岁成年人中观察到的 VE 最高(53.0%,95%CI:37.0 至 64.9)针对任何流感。仅在 2025/26 赛季(38.7%,95%CI:15.6 至 55.5)或 2024/25 和 2025/26 赛季(35.1%,95%CI:16.5 至 49.6)接种疫苗的调整后 VE 相似,但高于仅在 2024/25 赛季接种的疫苗(17.0%,B/维多利亚州的 95%CI:-16.1 至 40.7)。对于 甲型H3N2流感病毒,仅在 2025/26 赛季接种疫苗的人(27.9%,95%CI:11.5 至 41.2)的 VE 点估计值高于在两个赛季都接种疫苗的人(17.9%,95%CI:4.0 至 29.7)和仅在 2024/25 赛季接种疫苗的人(-6.1%,95%CI:-6.1%,95%CI: -28.7 至 12.5)。结论:我们的研究结果表明,季节性 疫苗接种 使门诊患者中实验室确诊的流感减少了约四分之一,18-59 岁成年人的 VE 较高。根据连续两个赛季的 疫苗接种 状态进行的 VE 估计显示 甲型H3N2流感病毒 和 B/Victoria 的模式不同。这些现实世界的发现支持了流感 疫苗接种 的持续公共卫生价值,并强调了持续病毒学 监测、预防和控制政策的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估中国北京 2025/26 季节末流感 疫苗 对 甲型H3N2流感病毒 和 B/Victoria 的有效性 (VE)。
设计与对象
我们采用测试阴性设计来评估2025年9月(第40/2025周)至2026年5月(第21/2026周)通过北京流感哨点病毒学监测系统入组的门诊流感样疾病(ILI)患者的流感VE。
核心方法
使用非条件多元逻辑回归模型估计 VE,调整年龄(建模为自然三次样条)、性别、合并症的存在、居住区域和日历周(建模为自然三次样条)。
主要结果
在 20,749 名接受检测的 ILI 患者中,4875 名(23.5%)流感呈阳性,其中 甲型H3N2流感病毒 和 B/Victoria 是主要毒株。
解释与创新
针对任何流感的调整后 VE 为 26.4%(95% CI:17.8 至 34.1),针对 甲型H3N2流感病毒 为 22.2%(95% CI:11.3 至 31.7),针对 B/Victoria 为 34.7%(95% CI:19.6 至 47.0),其中在 18-59 岁成年人中观察到的 VE 最高(53.0%,95%CI:37.0 至 64.9)针对任何流感。
科研与公卫意义
这些现实世界的发现支持了流感 疫苗接种 的持续公共卫生价值,并强调了持续病毒学 监测、预防和控制政策的重要性。
局限与证据强度
仅在 2025/26 赛季(38.7%,95%CI:15.6 至 55.5)或 2024/25 和 2025/26 赛季(35.1%,95%CI:16.5 至 49.6)接种疫苗的调整后 VE 相似,但高于仅在 2024/25 赛季接种的疫苗(17.0%,B/维多利亚州的 95%CI:-16.1 至 40.7)。

End-of-season influenza vaccine effectiveness against a(H3N2) and B/Victoria in Beijing, China, 2025/26 season.

Authors: Jiaojiao Zhang, Ying Shen, Weixian Shi, Jiaxin Ma, Jiachen Zhao, Chunna Ma, Dan Wu, Yingying Wang, Guilan Lu, Lu Zhang
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: This study aimed to estimate the end-of-season influenza vaccine effectiveness (VE) against A(H3N2) and B/Victoria in the 2025/26 season in Beijing, China. METHODS: We used a test-negative design to assess influenza VE among outpatients with influenza-like illness (ILI) enrolled through the Beijing influenza sentinel virological surveillance system from September 2025 (week 40/2025) to May 2026 (week 21/2026). Cases were ILI patients who tested positive for influenza; controls were those who tested negative. The VE was estimated using non-conditional multiple logistic regression models, adjusting for age (modelled as a natural cubic spline), sex, presence of comorbidities, area of residence, and calendar week (modelled as a natural cubic spline). RESULTS: Among 20,749 ILI patients tested, 4875 (23.5%) were positive for influenza, with A(H3N2) and B/Victoria being the predominant strains. Adjusted VE was 26.4% (95% CI: 17.8 to 34.1) against any influenza, 22.2% (95% CI: 11.3 to 31.7) against A(H3N2), and 34.7% (95% CI: 19.6 to 47.0) against B/Victoria, with the highest VE observed in adults aged 18-59 years (53.0%, 95%CI: 37.0 to 64.9) against any influenza. The adjusted VE was similar for those vaccinated in 2025/26 only (38.7%, 95%CI: 15.6 to 55.5) or both 2024/25 and 2025/26 seasons (35.1%, 95%CI: 16.5 to 49.6), but higher than those vaccinated in 2024/25 only (17.0%, 95%CI: -16.1 to 40.7) among B/Victoria. For A(H3N2), the VE point estimate was higher among those vaccinated in the 2025/26 season only (27.9%, 95%CI: 11.5 to 41.2) than among those vaccinated in both seasons (17.9%, 95%CI: 4.0 to 29.7) and 2024/25 only (-6.1%, 95%CI: -28.7 to 12.5). CONCLUSION: Our findings indicate that seasonal vaccination reduced laboratory-confirmed influenza among outpatients by approximately a quarter, with higher VE in adults aged 18-59 years. VE estimates according to vaccination status over two consecutive seasons showed different patterns for A(H3N2) and B/Victoria. These real-world findings support the ongoing public health value of influenza vaccination and highlight the importance of continuous virological surveillance, prevention, and control policy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to estimate the end-of-season influenza vaccine effectiveness (VE) against A(H3N2) and B/Victoria in the 2025/26 season in Beijing, China.
Design and population
We used a test-negative design to assess influenza VE among outpatients with influenza-like illness (ILI) enrolled through the Beijing influenza sentinel virological surveillance system from September 2025 (week 40/2025) to May 2026 (week 21/2026).
Core methods
The VE was estimated using non-conditional multiple logistic regression models, adjusting for age (modelled as a natural cubic spline), sex, presence of comorbidities, area of residence, and calendar week (modelled as a natural cubic spline).
Main results
Among 20,749 ILI patients tested, 4875 (23.5%) were positive for influenza, with A(H3N2) and B/Victoria being the predominant strains.

Adjusted VE was 26.4% (95% CI: 17.8 to 34.1) against any influenza, 22.2% (95% CI: 11.3 to 31.7) against A(H3N2), and 34.7% (95% CI: 19.6 to 47.0) against B/Victoria, with the highest VE observed in adults aged 18-59 years (53.0%, 95%CI: 37.0 to 64.9) against any influenza.

These real-world findings support the ongoing public health value of influenza vaccination and highlight the importance of continuous virological surveillance, prevention, and control policy.

The adjusted VE was similar for those vaccinated in 2025/26 only (38.7%, 95%CI: 15.6 to 55.5) or both 2024/25 and 2025/26 seasons (35.1%, 95%CI: 16.5 to 49.6), but higher than those vaccinated in 2024/25 only (17.0%, 95%CI: -16.1 to 40.7) among B/Victoria.

用于甲型流感病毒突变体快速原型制作的合成病毒学平台

A Synthetic Virology Platform for Rapid Prototyping of Influenza A Virus mutants
作者： Janarthan R. Murti, Karin Schön, Davide Angeletti
摘要中文翻译甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 仍然是一个重大的公共卫生威胁,有效的 疫苗 和 抗病毒药物 疗法的需求仍然是重中之重。体外测试对于研究新型 IAV 病毒及其免疫原性至关重要。为了满足这一需求,我们开发了一种合成病毒学方法来快速原型化并测试具有改变的抗原位点的 IAV 突变体。通过策略性地在双义质粒中插入限制性位点,我们生成了一种工具,可以无缝替换 1) 完整血凝素 (HA)、2) HA 球状头结构域以生成头/茎嵌合体或 3) 五个典型抗原位点。克隆插入片段可以通过计算设计和人工合成,从而能够拯救合成甲型流感病毒。使用该平台,我们拯救了 IAV H1N1、H3N2 和 H5N1,并继续仅使用病毒 基因组 序列制作了它们各自的抗原位点突变体和嵌合 IAV 原型。该平台可用于生成 疫苗 种子菌株或研究抗病毒免疫。

A Synthetic Virology Platform for Rapid Prototyping of Influenza A Virus mutants

Authors: Janarthan R. Murti, Karin Schön, Davide Angeletti
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A virus (IAV) remains a significant public health threat, with the need for effective vaccines and antiviral therapies remaining a high priority. In vitro testing is paramount in studying novel IAV viruses and their immunogenicity. To address this need, we developed a synthetic virology approach to rapidly prototype and test IAV mutants with altered antigenic sites. By strategically inserting restriction sites within an ambisense plasmid, we generated a tool where we can seamlessly replace either 1) the full hemagglutinin (HA), 2) the HA globular head domain to generate head/stalk chimeras or 3) the five canonical antigenic sites. The cloning inserts can be computationally designed and artificially synthesized, enabling rescue of synthetic Influenza A viruses. Using this platform we rescued IAV H1N1, H3N2 & H5N1, and went on to prototype their respective antigenic site mutants & chimeric IAVs using viral genome sequence alone. This platform could be useful for generating vaccine seed strains or to study anti-viral immunity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
To address this need, we developed a synthetic virology approach to rapidly prototype and test IAV mutants with altered antigenic sites.
Design and population
This platform could be useful for generating vaccine seed strains or to study anti-viral immunity.
Core methods
Using this platform we rescued IAV H1N1, H3N2 & H5N1, and went on to prototype their respective antigenic site mutants & chimeric IAVs using viral genome sequence alone.
Main results
Influenza A virus (IAV) remains a significant public health threat, with the need for effective vaccines and antiviral therapies remaining a high priority.
Interpretation and novelty
In vitro testing is paramount in studying novel IAV viruses and their immunogenicity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The cloning inserts can be computationally designed and artificially synthesized, enabling rescue of synthetic Influenza A viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
This deterministic extraction is based on retrieved fulltext evidence. The source does not explicitly report enough limitations for an independent appraisal, so confidence is low.

对照表:抗病毒药物 药物为 季节性流感。

Comparison chart: Antiviral drugs for seasonal influenza.
作者： Authors unavailable
摘要中文翻译流感通常是一种自限性疾病,但可能会出现肺炎、呼吸衰竭和死亡等并发症,尤其是高危人群(见表 1)。流感 发病率 降低了实验室确诊流感的 发病率 以及与流感疾病相关的并发症的风险。只要流感病毒在社区中传播,就应向所有≥6 个月大的人提供年度 疫苗接种。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在比较 抗病毒药物 药物与 季节性流感,解决对有效 治疗 方案的需求,以减少肺炎、呼吸衰竭和死亡等并发症,特别是在高危人群中。
设计与对象
该研究是抗病毒药物药物与季节性流感的比较图,重点关注人类流感甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09、甲型H3N2流感病毒以及B/Victoria和B/Yamagata病毒。它包括所有≥6 个月大、并发症风险增加的人。
核心方法
这些方法包括审查实验室确诊的流感病例,并分析年度 发病率 在减少流感 发病率 和相关并发症方面的有效性。
主要结果
结果表明,流感 发病率 降低了实验室确诊流感的 发病率 以及与流感疾病相关的并发症的风险。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明每年一次的 疫苗接种 是减轻 季节性流感 负担的有效策略,特别是在高危人群中。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了年度 疫苗接种 在预防 季节性流感 及其并发症方面的重要性,为公共卫生策略提供了信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对观察数据的依赖而受到限制,并且不包括详细的统计分析。然而,由于其重点关注实验室确诊病例和高危人群,因此证据有力。

Comparison chart: Antiviral drugs for seasonal influenza.

Authors: Authors unavailable
Original abstract (English)
Influenza is typically a self-limited illness, but complications including pneumonia, respiratory failure, and death can occur, especially in persons at increased risk (see Table 1). Influenza vaccination reduces the incidence of laboratory-confirmed influenza and the risk of complications associated with influenza illness. Annual vaccination should be offered to all persons ≥6 months old as long as influenza viruses are circulating in the community.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to compare antiviral drugs for seasonal influenza, addressing the need for effective treatment options to reduce complications such as pneumonia, respiratory failure, and death, particularly in high-risk populations.
Design and population
The study was a comparison chart of antiviral drugs for seasonal influenza, focusing on human influenza A(H1N1)pdm09, A(H3N2), and B/Victoria and B/Yamagata viruses. It included all persons ≥6 months old who are at increased risk of complications.
Core methods
The methods involved reviewing laboratory-confirmed influenza cases and analyzing the effectiveness of annual vaccination in reducing the incidence of influenza and associated complications.
Main results
The results indicated that influenza vaccination reduces the incidence of laboratory-confirmed influenza and the risk of complications associated with influenza illness.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence that annual vaccination is an effective strategy for reducing the burden of seasonal influenza, particularly in high-risk groups.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform public health strategies by highlighting the importance of annual vaccination in preventing seasonal influenza and its complications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on observational data and does not include detailed statistical analyses. However, the evidence is strong due to its focus on laboratory-confirmed cases and high-risk populations.

通过数字 PCR 区分废水中的禽病毒和 季节性流感 病毒。

Differentiating Avian and Seasonal Influenza Viruses in Wastewater by Digital PCR.
作者： Valentina Paracchini, Mauro Petrillo, Francisco Mayo Leon, Gerhard Buttinger, Sabrina Marciano, Marika Crimaudo, Calogero Terregino, Carolina Veneri, Giuseppina La Rosa, Elisabetta Suffredini
摘要中文翻译高致病性甲型禽流感 (H5Nx) 病毒,特别是进化枝 2.3.4.4b,对全球健康构成重大风险。我们组合并试验了 2 个双链逆转录数字 PCR,以区分废水中的禽类病毒和 季节性流感 病毒。这种方法能够实现快速、毒株特异性检测,以减少流感病毒环境 监测 的模糊性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高致病性甲型禽流感 (H5Nx) 病毒,特别是进化枝 2.3.4.4b,对全球健康构成重大风险。该研究旨在解决准确区分废水中禽类病毒和 季节性流感 病毒的需求。
设计与对象
该研究结合并试验了 2 个双链逆转录数字 PCR,以区分废水中的禽类病毒和 季节性流感 病毒。它专注于流感病毒的环境 监测,重点是人类流感病毒株。
核心方法
这种方法能够实现快速、毒株特异性检测,以减少流感病毒环境 监测 的模糊性。这些方法涉及使用数字 PCR 来检测和区分废水样品中的禽类病毒和 季节性流感 病毒。
主要结果
该研究证明了使用数字 PCR 检测和区分废水样品中的禽类病毒和 季节性流感 病毒的可行性。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为一种环境 监测 的新方法,可以提高检测废水中流感病毒的准确性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为针对 季节性流感 病毒的更有效的基于废水的 监测 策略提供信息,从而加强公共卫生监测和应对。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其关注特定方法及其在受控环境中的应用而受到限制。由于研究范围有限,证据强度中等。

Differentiating Avian and Seasonal Influenza Viruses in Wastewater by Digital PCR.

Authors: Valentina Paracchini, Mauro Petrillo, Francisco Mayo Leon, Gerhard Buttinger, Sabrina Marciano, Marika Crimaudo, Calogero Terregino, Carolina Veneri, Giuseppina La Rosa, Elisabetta Suffredini
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5Nx) viruses, particularly clade 2.3.4.4b, pose substantial global health risks. We combined and piloted 2 duplex reverse transcription digital PCRs to discriminate between avian and seasonal influenza viruses in wastewater. This approach enabled rapid, strain-specific detection to reduce ambiguity in environmental surveillance for influenza viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5Nx) viruses, particularly clade 2.3.4.4b, pose substantial global health risks. The study aimed to address the need for accurate differentiation between avian and seasonal influenza viruses in wastewater.
Design and population
The study combined and piloted 2 duplex reverse transcription digital PCRs to discriminate between avian and seasonal influenza viruses in wastewater. It focused on environmental surveillance for influenza viruses, with an emphasis on human influenza strains.
Core methods
This approach enabled rapid, strain-specific detection to reduce ambiguity in environmental surveillance for influenza viruses. The methods involved the use of digital PCR to detect and differentiate between avian and seasonal influenza viruses in wastewater samples.
Main results
The study demonstrated the feasibility of using digital PCR to detect and differentiate between avian and seasonal influenza viruses in wastewater samples.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as a novel method for environmental surveillance that can improve the accuracy of detecting influenza viruses in wastewater.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform more effective wastewater-based surveillance strategies for seasonal influenza viruses, enhancing public health monitoring and response.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a specific method and its application in a controlled setting. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the limited scope of the study.

表位引导的优化提高了基于 NA 的 mRNA 疫苗 对 季节性流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 病毒的保护功效。

Epitope-guided optimization improves the protective efficacy of NA-based mRNA vaccines against seasonal influenza A(H3N2) virus.
作者： Hyunbeen Kim, Soyoung Lee, Seunghye Cho, Woojin Shin, Atanas V. Demirev, Taeyoung Lee, You-Jin Kim, Dokeun Kim, Sae Am Song, Kyung Ran Jun
摘要中文翻译神经氨酸酶 (NA) 越来越被认为是一种有前景的靶抗原,可用于改善流感 疫苗 的广度和持久性,但相对于基于血凝素的方法,基于 NA 的 mRNA 疫苗 仍未得到探索。在这里,我们将计算引导的 NA 抗原设计策略应用于季节性 H3N2 流感病毒,并研究表位引导的优化是否可以提高基于 NA 的 mRNA 疫苗 的免疫原性和保护功效。NA-D3 是一种根据流行的 H3N2 病毒的进化特征设计的 NA 抗原,首先在 mRNA 疫苗 中进行评估。尽管没有观察到可检测的神经氨酸酶抑制 (NAI) 活性,但该 疫苗 提供了针对 H3N2 攻击的部分保护并减少了肺部病毒复制。为了进一步提高 NA 抗原的免疫原性,预测的 B 细胞和 T 细胞表位被纳入抗原设计过程中,从而产生了三个连续优化的 NA 候选物:F0、F1 和 F2。在随后的攻击实验中,F1 和 F2 提供了完全的保护,实现了 100% 的存活,并且与对照组相比,肺部的病毒 RNA 水平和传染性病毒滴度显着降低。优化的 NA mRNA 疫苗 还诱导了针对异源 H3N2 病毒的可检测 NAI 活性,尽管水平相对较低。总的来说,这些发现证明了基于 NA 的 mRNA 疫苗接种 对 H3N2 流感病毒的保护潜力,并在 NA 抗原优化中建立了迭代、表位引导的策略 - 将计算预测与实验验证相结合 - 作为开发广泛保护性的基于 NA 的 mRNA 疫苗 的有效方法。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探索神经氨酸酶 (NA) 作为提高流感 疫苗 功效的靶抗原的潜力,特别是对于季节性 H3N2 流感病毒,因为与基于血凝素的方法相比,基于 NA 的 mRNA 疫苗 尚未得到探索。
设计与对象
该研究在涉及小鼠的攻击实验中评估了基于 NA 的 mRNA 疫苗 对季节性 H3N2 流感病毒的保护功效。
核心方法
研究人员根据循环H3N2病毒的进化特征设计了NA抗原(NA-D3),并将预测的B细胞和T细胞表位纳入抗原设计过程中,依次优化了三种NA候选物(F0、F1和F2)。
主要结果
优化的 NA mRNA 疫苗,特别是 F1 和 F2,提供了针对 H3N2 攻击的完全保护,减少了肺部的病毒复制,并诱导可检测到的针对异源 H3N2 病毒的神经氨酸酶抑制 (NAI) 活性。
解释与创新
研究结果证明了基于 NA 的 mRNA 疫苗 对 H3N2 流感病毒的保护潜力,并建立了一种表位引导策略来优化 NA 抗原,将计算预测与实验验证相结合。
科研与公卫意义
优化的 NA mRNA 疫苗 有助于开发具有广泛保护性的基于 NA 的 mRNA 疫苗,从而增强流感 疫苗接种 策略的广度和持久性。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括使用单一动物模型以及需要在人体临床试验中进一步验证。由于样本量有限且使用非人类模型,证据强度中等。

Epitope-guided optimization improves the protective efficacy of NA-based mRNA vaccines against seasonal influenza A(H3N2) virus.

Authors: Hyunbeen Kim, Soyoung Lee, Seunghye Cho, Woojin Shin, Atanas V. Demirev, Taeyoung Lee, You-Jin Kim, Dokeun Kim, Sae Am Song, Kyung Ran Jun
Original abstract (English)
Neuraminidase (NA) is increasingly recognized as a promising target antigen for improving the breadth and durability of influenza vaccines, yet NA-based mRNA vaccines remain comparatively unexplored relative to hemagglutinin-based approaches. Here, we applied a computationally guided NA antigen-design strategy to seasonal H3N2 influenza viruses and investigated whether epitope-guided optimization could improve the immunogenicity and protective efficacy of NA-based mRNA vaccines. NA-D3, an NA antigen designed based on the evolutionary characteristics of circulating H3N2 viruses, was first evaluated in an mRNA vaccine. This vaccine provided partial protection against H3N2 challenge and reduced viral replication in the lungs, although detectable neuraminidase inhibition (NAI) activity was not observed. To further improve NA antigen immunogenicity, predicted B- and T-cell epitopes were incorporated into the antigen design process, resulting in three sequentially optimized NA candidates, F0, F1, and F2. In a subsequent challenge experiment, F1 and F2 conferred complete protection, achieving 100% survival and significantly lower viral RNA levels and infectious viral titers in the lungs compared to controls. The optimized NA mRNA vaccines also induced detectable NAI activity against heterologous H3N2 viruses, albeit at relatively modest levels. Collectively, these findings demonstrate the protective potential of NA-based mRNA vaccination against H3N2 influenza viruses and establish iterative, epitope-guided strategy in NA antigen optimization - integrating computational prediction with experimental validation - as an effective approach for developing broadly protective NA-based mRNA vaccines.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the potential of neuraminidase (NA) as a target antigen for improving influenza vaccine efficacy, particularly for seasonal H3N2 influenza viruses, as NA-based mRNA vaccines have been relatively unexplored compared to hemagglutinin-based approaches.
Design and population
The study evaluated the protective efficacy of NA-based mRNA vaccines against seasonal H3N2 influenza viruses in a challenge experiment involving mice.
Core methods
The researchers designed an NA antigen (NA-D3) based on the evolutionary characteristics of circulating H3N2 viruses and incorporated predicted B- and T-cell epitopes into the antigen design process to sequentially optimize three NA candidates (F0, F1, and F2).
Main results
The optimized NA mRNA vaccines, particularly F1 and F2, provided complete protection against H3N2 challenge, reducing viral replication in the lungs and inducing detectable neuraminidase inhibition (NAI) activity against heterologous H3N2 viruses.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate the protective potential of NA-based mRNA vaccines against H3N2 influenza viruses and establish an epitope-guided strategy for optimizing NA antigens, integrating computational prediction with experimental validation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The optimized NA mRNA vaccines could contribute to the development of broadly protective NA-based mRNA vaccines, enhancing the breadth and durability of influenza vaccination strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the use of a single animal model and the need for further validation in human clinical trials. The evidence strength is moderate due to the limited sample size and the use of a non-human model.

牛粘病毒抗性蛋白 (Mx)1(而非 Mx2)介导 抗病毒药物 针对人类和禽甲型流感病毒(包括 H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b)的活性。

Bovine myxovirus resistance protein (Mx)1, but not Mx2, mediates antiviral activity against human and avian influenza A viruses, including H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b.
作者： Rubaiyea Farrukee, Yongyan Xia, Rachel Morris, James B. Barnes, Clyde Dapat, Saira Hussain, Yi-Mo Deng, Victoria Meliopoulos, Stacey Schultz-Cherry, Sarah L. Londrigan
摘要中文翻译高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 分支 2.3.4.4b 病毒最近感染了美国的奶牛,引起了对人类 跨物种溢出 的担忧。牛 宿主 防御因子(包括粘病毒抗性蛋白(bMx1 和 bMx2))的 抗病毒药物 作用仍相对未得到充分研究。我们检测了 HPAI H5N1 感染后分化牛细胞中 bMx1/bMx2 的诱导,并使用过表达、敲低和诱变方法评估了它们的 抗病毒药物 活性。bMx1 和 bMx2 在乳腺细胞中的诱导作用比在气管细胞中的诱导作用更强。bMx1 的过表达(而非 bMx2)显着抑制 季节性流感 A 病毒 (IAV)、低致病性和高致病性禽流感病毒,包括 H5N1 2.3.4.4b,但不能抑制 B 型流感病毒。相反,bMx1 的敲低导致 IAV 生长增强。此外,bMx1 抑制病毒复制的早期阶段,从而减少病毒转录和病毒复制周期的后续步骤。已知的 突变 可以降低人类 MxA (hMxA) 活性,同时也会损害 bMx1 功能,而与 hMxA 抗性相关的病毒核蛋白 (NP) 中的 突变 也会降低病毒对 bMx1 的敏感性。此外,系统发育分析表明,一小部分(>0.03%)禽类 H5N1 2.3.4.4b 病毒已经携带其中一些 NP 突变。这些发现表明,虽然目前的 H5N1 病毒在很大程度上仍然对 bMx1 敏感,但病毒 进化 或机会性重配可能会导致 Mx 耐药性并增加大流行风险。重要性高致病性禽流感病毒已蔓延到奶牛中,并自 2024 年以来一直在美国奶牛场传播。奶牛对流感病毒的先天 抗病毒药物 免疫应答 尚未得到广泛研究。在这项研究中,我们重点关注 Mx 蛋白,它是针对病毒的先天免疫防御系统的关键部分。我们专门研究了牛 Mx 蛋白,并证明了 抗病毒药物 对不同流感病毒(包括高致病性毒株)的活性。我们还表明,牛 Mx1 的 抗病毒药物 活性机制与人类等效的 MxA 蛋白非常相似。此外,我们发现这些禽类病毒中的一小部分具有与牛 Mx1 和人 MxA 蛋白抗性相关的 突变。总体而言,这项工作强调了高致病性流感病毒通过在牛(一种与人类关系密切的哺乳动物物种)中持续传播而增加的大流行风险。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解牛粘病毒抗性蛋白(bMx1和bMx2)在应对高致病性禽流感(HPAI)H5N1分支2.3.4.4b病毒中的抗病毒药物作用,该病毒最近感染了美国的奶牛,引起了对人类跨物种溢出的担忧。知识差距在于牛对流感病毒的先天 抗病毒药物 免疫应答 的研究不足,特别是 Mx 蛋白的作用。
设计与对象
该研究涉及检查 HPAI H5N1 感染后分化牛细胞中 bMx1/bMx2 的诱导情况,并使用过表达、敲低和诱变方法评估其 抗病毒药物 活性。该研究是利用牛乳腺和气管细胞在体外进行的。
核心方法
这些方法包括检查牛细胞中的 bMx1/bMx2 诱导、bMx1 和 bMx2 的过度表达、敲低和诱变,以及评估 抗病毒药物 针对不同流感病毒(包括 H5N1 2.3.4.4b)的活性。
主要结果
bMx1 和 bMx2 在乳腺细胞中的诱导作用比在气管细胞中的诱导作用更强。bMx1 的过表达(而非 bMx2)显着抑制 季节性流感 A 病毒 (IAV)、低致病性和高致病性禽流感病毒,包括 H5N1 2.3.4.4b,但不能抑制 B 型流感病毒。bMx1 的敲低导致 IAV 生长增强。bMx1 抑制病毒复制的早期阶段,从而减少病毒转录和病毒复制周期的后续步骤。少数禽类 H5N1 2.3.4.4b 病毒已携带可降低 MxA 活性的 突变。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,虽然目前的 H5N1 病毒在很大程度上仍然对 bMx1 敏感,但病毒 进化 或重配可能会导致 Mx 耐药性并增加大流行风险。牛Mx1的抗病毒药物活性机制与人类等效的MxA蛋白非常相似,并且少数禽类病毒具有与牛Mx1和人MxA蛋白的抗性相关的突变。
科研与公卫意义
这项工作强调了高致病性流感病毒通过在牛(一种与人类关系密切的哺乳动物物种)中持续传播而增加的大流行风险。这些发现对牛流感的 监测、诊断 和 治疗 以及潜在的人类 跨物种溢出 事件具有影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到实验的体外性质和牛细胞的使用的限制,可能无法完全代表体内情况。病毒 突变 分析的样本量较小,研究结果对其他流感病毒和物种的普遍性尚不确定。证据强度中等。

Bovine myxovirus resistance protein (Mx)1, but not Mx2, mediates antiviral activity against human and avian influenza A viruses, including H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b.

Authors: Rubaiyea Farrukee, Yongyan Xia, Rachel Morris, James B. Barnes, Clyde Dapat, Saira Hussain, Yi-Mo Deng, Victoria Meliopoulos, Stacey Schultz-Cherry, Sarah L. Londrigan
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses have recently infected dairy cows in the United States, raising concerns about human spillover. The antiviral roles of bovine host defense factors, including myxovirus resistance proteins (bMx1 and bMx2), remain relatively understudied. We examined bMx1/bMx2 induction in differentiated bovine cells following HPAI H5N1 infection and assessed their antiviral activity using overexpression, knockdown, and mutagenesis approaches. Both bMx1 and bMx2 were more strongly induced in mammary cells than in tracheal cells. Overexpression of bMx1, but not bMx2, significantly inhibited seasonal influenza A viruses (IAVs), low-pathogenic, and highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses, including H5N1 2.3.4.4b, but not influenza B viruses. Conversely, knockdown of bMx1 resulted in enhanced IAV growth. Further, bMx1 inhibited early stages of viral replication, acting to reduce viral transcription and subsequent steps in the viral replication cycle. Known mutations that reduce human MxA (hMxA) activity also impaired bMx1 function, and mutations in the viral nucleoprotein (NP) associated with resistance to hMxA also reduced virus sensitivity to bMx1. Furthermore, phylogenetic analysis showed that a small minority (>0.03%) of avian H5N1 2.3.4.4b viruses already carry some of these NP mutations. These findings suggest that while current H5N1 viruses largely remain susceptible to bMx1, viral evolution or opportunistic reassortment could drive Mx resistance and increase pandemic risk.IMPORTANCEHighly pathogenic avian influenza viruses have spilled over into dairy cows and have been circulating on dairy farms in the United States since 2024. The innate antiviral immune responses of cows to influenza viruses have not been extensively studied. In this study, we focused on Mx proteins, a key part of the innate immune defense system toward viruses. We specifically looked at bovine Mx proteins and demonstrated antiviral activity toward different influenza viruses, including the highly pathogenic strains. We also showed that the mechanism of antiviral activity of bovine Mx1 was very similar to that of the human equivalent MxA protein. Further, we found that a small minority of these avian viruses have mutations associated with resistance to both bovine Mx1 and human MxA proteins. Overall, this work highlights the elevated pandemic risk for highly pathogenic influenza viruses through continual circulation in cows, a mammalian species in close proximity to humans.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the antiviral roles of bovine myxovirus resistance proteins (bMx1 and bMx2) in response to highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses, which have recently infected dairy cows in the United States, raising concerns about human spillover. The knowledge gap lies in the understudied innate antiviral immune responses of cows to influenza viruses, particularly the roles of Mx proteins.
Design and population
The study involved examining bMx1/bMx2 induction in differentiated bovine cells following HPAI H5N1 infection and assessing their antiviral activity using overexpression, knockdown, and mutagenesis approaches. The research was conducted in vitro using bovine mammary and tracheal cells.
Core methods
The methods included examining bMx1/bMx2 induction in bovine cells, overexpression, knockdown, and mutagenesis of bMx1 and bMx2, and assessing antiviral activity against different influenza viruses, including H5N1 2.3.4.4b.
Main results
Both bMx1 and bMx2 were more strongly induced in mammary cells than in tracheal cells. Overexpression of bMx1, but not bMx2, significantly inhibited seasonal influenza A viruses (IAVs), low-pathogenic, and highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses, including H5N1 2.3.4.4b, but not influenza B viruses. Knockdown of bMx1 resulted in enhanced IAV growth. bMx1 inhibited early stages of viral replication, acting to reduce viral transcription and subsequent steps in the viral replication cycle. A small minority of avian H5N1 2.3.4.4b viruses already carry mutations that reduce MxA activity.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that while current H5N1 viruses largely remain susceptible to bMx1, viral evolution or reassortment could drive Mx resistance and increase pandemic risk. The mechanism of antiviral activity of bovine Mx1 was very similar to that of the human equivalent MxA protein, and a small minority of avian viruses have mutations associated with resistance to both bovine Mx1 and human MxA proteins.
Scientific and public-health significance
This work highlights the elevated pandemic risk for highly pathogenic influenza viruses through continual circulation in cows, a mammalian species in close proximity to humans. The findings have implications for surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment of influenza in cattle and potential human spillover events.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the in vitro nature of the experiments and the use of bovine cells, which may not fully represent the in vivo situation. The sample size for the viral mutation analysis was small, and the generalizability of the findings to other influenza viruses and species is uncertain. The evidence strength is moderate.

ADAR 介导的甲型流感病毒 基因组 的 RNA 编辑可能具有年龄依赖性,并且与病毒载量呈负相关。

ADAR-mediated RNA editing of Influenza A virus genomes may be age-dependent and inversely correlates with viral load.
作者： Julia Widström, Joshua Fingal, Johan Ringlander, Magnus Lindh, Maria Andersson, Martina Wahllöf, Gustaf E Rydell, Luisa Fernanda Bustamante Jaramillo, Michael Kann, Johan Westin
摘要中文翻译背景:季节性流感 流行病导致严重的 死亡率,特别是在老年人中。甲型流感病毒通过多种机制触发 宿主 先天免疫系统。属于 RNA 作用腺苷脱氨酶 (ADAR) 组的人类酶在先天 抗病毒药物 免疫应答 中发挥作用,部分是通过在病毒 RNA 中诱导 突变 来发挥作用。这种机制在甲型流感病毒感染中对人类的影响仍然无法解释。目的:本研究的目的是评估 ADAR 诱导的 突变 对住院患者甲型流感病毒的临床影响。方法:对 75 名年龄在 41 岁至 95 岁之间、感染 甲型H3N2流感病毒 流感病毒的住院患者的呼吸道样本进行了全 基因组 深度测序。分析了少数病毒亚群中与 ADAR 相关的 突变 与人口统计学和临床​​因素的关系。结果:ADAR 相关的 突变 显示与病毒载量 (p < 0.0001) 和年龄 (p = 0.0051) 呈显着负相关,尤其是在 75 岁以上的患者中。ADAR 相关 突变 与病毒载量之间的关联与年龄、炎症和疾病严重程度无关 (p < 0.001)。结论:我们发现 ADAR 诱导的 突变 与病毒载量之间存在关联,这可能表明 ADAR 对甲型流感病毒具有 抗病毒药物 功能。老年人中较低的 突变 率和相关的较高病毒载量表明 ADAR 活性随年龄而降低,这可能导致疾病严重程度增加。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 ADAR 诱导的 突变 对住院患者中甲型流感病毒的临床影响,解决该机制在人类中无法解释的影响。季节性流感流行病引起显着的死亡率,特别是在老年人中,甲型流感病毒通过多种机制触发宿主先天免疫系统。ADAR 等人类酶通过诱导病毒 RNA 中的 突变 在先天 抗病毒药物 免疫应答 中发挥作用。
设计与对象
该研究对 75 名年龄在 41 岁至 95 岁之间、感染 甲型H3N2流感病毒 病毒的住院患者的 A 型流感病毒进行了全面的 基因组 深度测序,以分析 ADAR 相关的 甲型H3N2流感病毒 与人口统计学和临床​​因素的关系。
核心方法
对 75 名年龄在 41 岁至 95 岁之间、感染 甲型H3N2流感病毒 流感病毒的住院患者的呼吸道样本进行了全 基因组 深度测序。分析了少数病毒亚群中与 ADAR 相关的 突变 与人口统计学和临床​​因素的关系。
主要结果
ADAR 相关的 突变 显示与病毒载量 (p < 0.0001) 和年龄 (p = 0.0051) 呈显着负相关,尤其是在 75 岁以上的患者中。ADAR 相关 突变 与病毒载量之间的关联与年龄、炎症和疾病严重程度无关 (p < 0.001)。
解释与创新
我们发现 ADAR 诱导的 突变 与病毒载量之间存在关联,这可能表明 ADAR 对甲型流感病毒具有 抗病毒药物 功能。老年人中较低的 突变 率和相关的较高病毒载量表明 ADAR 活性随年龄而降低,这可能导致疾病严重程度增加。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明 ADAR 活性可能在甲型流感病毒感染的临床结果中发挥作用,特别是在老年人中。这可以为 监测、诊断 和 治疗 流感策略提供信息,特别是在高危人群中。
局限与证据强度
该研究因样本量小且只关注单一流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 毒株而受到限制。研究结果可能无法推广到其他流感毒株或人群。研究设计不允许因果推断,结果基于观察数据。

ADAR-mediated RNA editing of Influenza A virus genomes may be age-dependent and inversely correlates with viral load.

Authors: Julia Widström, Joshua Fingal, Johan Ringlander, Magnus Lindh, Maria Andersson, Martina Wahllöf, Gustaf E Rydell, Luisa Fernanda Bustamante Jaramillo, Michael Kann, Johan Westin
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Seasonal influenza epidemics cause significant mortality, especially among the elderly. Influenza A virus triggers the host innate immune system through various mechanisms. Human enzymes belonging to the group of RNA-acting adenosine deaminases (ADARs) play a role in the innate antiviral immune response, partly by inducing mutations in viral RNA. The effect of this mechanismin influenza A virus infection remains unexplained in humans. OBJECTIVE: The aim of this study was to assess the clinical impact of ADAR-induced mutations on influenza A virus in hospitalised patients. METHODS: Full-genome deep sequencing of Influenza A virus was performed on respiratory samples from 75 hospitalised, Influenza A(H3N2) virus infected patients, aged 41 to 95 years. ADAR-associated mutations in minor viral subpopulations were analysed in relation to demographic and clinical factors. RESULTS: ADAR-associated mutations showed a significant inverse correlation with viral load (p < 0.0001) and age (p = 0.0051), especially in patients older than 75 years. The association between ADAR-associated mutations and viral load was independent of age, inflammation, and disease severity (p < 0.001). CONCLUSIONS: We found an association between ADAR-induced mutations and viral load, which may indicate an antiviral function of ADAR on influenza A virus. The lower mutation rate and associated higher viral load in the elderly suggest an age-dependent reduced ADAR activity, which may contribute to increased disease severity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the clinical impact of ADAR-induced mutations on influenza A virus in hospitalised patients, addressing the unexplained effect of this mechanism in humans. Seasonal influenza epidemics cause significant mortality, especially among the elderly, and influenza A virus triggers the host innate immune system through various mechanisms. Human enzymes, such as ADARs, play a role in the innate antiviral immune response by inducing mutations in viral RNA.
Design and population
The study involved full-genome deep sequencing of Influenza A virus from 75 hospitalised, Influenza A(H3N2) virus infected patients, aged 41 to 95 years, to analyse ADAR-associated mutations in relation to demographic and clinical factors.
Core methods
Full-genome deep sequencing of Influenza A virus was performed on respiratory samples from 75 hospitalised, Influenza A(H3N2) virus infected patients, aged 41 to 95 years. ADAR-associated mutations in minor viral subpopulations were analysed in relation to demographic and clinical factors.
Main results
ADAR-associated mutations showed a significant inverse correlation with viral load (p < 0.0001) and age (p = 0.0051), especially in patients older than 75 years. The association between ADAR-associated mutations and viral load was independent of age, inflammation, and disease severity (p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
We found an association between ADAR-induced mutations and viral load, which may indicate an antiviral function of ADAR on influenza A virus. The lower mutation rate and associated higher viral load in the elderly suggest an age-dependent reduced ADAR activity, which may contribute to increased disease severity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that ADAR activity may play a role in the clinical outcome of influenza A virus infection, particularly in the elderly. This could inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies for influenza, especially in high-risk populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the small sample size and the focus on a single influenza A(H3N2) strain. The findings may not be generalisable to other influenza strains or populations. The study design does not allow for causal inference, and the results are based on observational data.

美国猪人界面展览会上的甲型流感病毒

Influenza A Virus at the Exhibition Swine‐Human Interface in the USA
作者： Dillon S. McBride, Martha I. Nelson, Hannah J. Cochran, Heather Norris Nicholson, Elizabeth M. Ohl, Yunho Jang, Natosha Zanders, Dan Cui, Han Di, Charles Todd Davis
摘要中文翻译简介:在美国,人畜共患甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 传播 经常发生在在农业竞赛中展示猪的青少年中,存在持续的大流行风险。然而,这一高危人群缺乏活跃的 监测。方法:为了弥补这一差距,我们在 2019-2021 年期间招募了 9 场生猪展览的 711 名青年参与者,并收集了 700 份鼻拭子和 400 份血清样本。我们评估了参与者针对世界卫生组织推荐的八种甲型流感 H1 和 H3 变异候选 疫苗 病毒 (CVV) 的中和抗体滴度。结果:我们发现最高的中和滴度不一定与同一时期展览猪中最流行的特定病毒相关。队列研究 的几何平均滴度相对于 IV-A 簇(进化枝 3.1990.4.a)CVV 较低,我们根据猪中同步 IAV 监测(n = 430 基因组 序列)确定该簇是 2020-2021 年展览猪中最流行的 HA 谱系。结论:在这一频繁接触牲畜的高危人群中,中和抗体水平相对较低,表明需要持续的外展和监测。对猪和人类 IAV 的持续监测可以为病毒风险评估提供信息,并为候选 疫苗 病毒的选择提供考虑因素。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在美国,人畜共患甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 传播 经常发生在在农业竞赛中展示猪的青少年中,存在持续的大流行风险。
设计与对象
2019-2021年,我们在九场生猪展览中招募了711名青年参与者,收集了700份鼻拭子和400份血清样本。
核心方法
为了解决这一差距,我们评估了参与者针对世界卫生组织推荐的八种甲型流感 H1 和 H3 变异候选 疫苗 病毒 (CVV) 的中和抗体滴度。
主要结果
我们发现最高的中和滴度不一定与同一时期展览猪中最流行的特定病毒相关。
解释与创新
在这一频繁接触牲畜的高危人群中,中和抗体水平相对较低,表明需要持续的外展和监测。
科研与公卫意义
对猪和人类 IAV 的持续监测可以为病毒风险评估提供信息,并为候选 疫苗 病毒的选择提供考虑因素。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计或证据强度评估的具体限制。

Influenza A Virus at the Exhibition Swine‐Human Interface in the USA

Authors: Dillon S. McBride, Martha I. Nelson, Hannah J. Cochran, Heather Norris Nicholson, Elizabeth M. Ohl, Yunho Jang, Natosha Zanders, Dan Cui, Han Di, Charles Todd Davis
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: In the USA, zoonotic influenza A virus (IAV) transmission occurs frequently in youth who exhibit swine at agricultural competitions, presenting an ongoing pandemic risk. However, active surveillance in this high-risk human population is lacking. METHODS: To address this gap, we enrolled 711 youth participants at nine swine exhibitions during 2019-2021 and collected 700 nasal swabs and 400 serum samples. We assessed the neutralizing antibody titers of enrollees against eight influenza A H1 and H3 variant candidate vaccine viruses (CVVs) recommended by the World Health Organization. RESULTS: We found that the highest neutralizing titers are not necessarily associated with the specific viruses which are most prevalent in exhibition swine during the same time period. The geometric mean titers of the cohort were low against the Cluster IV-A (clade 3.1990.4.a) CVV, which we determined to be the most prevalent HA lineage circulating in exhibition swine during 2020-2021 based on simultaneous IAV surveillance in swine (n = 430 genome sequences). CONCLUSIONS: The relatively low neutralizing antibody levels in this high-risk population with intense exposure to livestock indicates that sustained outreach and monitoring is needed. Continued monitoring of IAVs in both swine and humans can inform viral risk assessments and provide considerations for candidate vaccine virus selection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
In the USA, zoonotic influenza A virus (IAV) transmission occurs frequently in youth who exhibit swine at agricultural competitions, presenting an ongoing pandemic risk.
Design and population
We enrolled 711 youth participants at nine swine exhibitions during 2019-2021 and collected 700 nasal swabs and 400 serum samples.
Core methods
To address this gap, we assessed the neutralizing antibody titers of enrollees against eight influenza A H1 and H3 variant candidate vaccine viruses (CVVs) recommended by the World Health Organization.
Main results
We found that the highest neutralizing titers are not necessarily associated with the specific viruses which are most prevalent in exhibition swine during the same time period.
Interpretation and novelty
The relatively low neutralizing antibody levels in this high-risk population with intense exposure to livestock indicates that sustained outreach and monitoring is needed.
Scientific and public-health significance
Continued monitoring of IAVs in both swine and humans can inform viral risk assessments and provide considerations for candidate vaccine virus selection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations to the study design or evidence strength assessment.

基于结构的蓝藻代谢物针对甲型流感病毒关键靶点的虚拟筛选

Structure-based virtual screening of cyanobacterial metabolites against key influenza A virus targets
作者： Mohanakrishnan Neelakrishnan, Brahmaiah Pendyala, Akhil Rautela, Vivek Grandhe, Ankit Patras, Mohana Krishnan Neelakrishnan
摘要中文翻译甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 仍然是全球健康和畜牧业的主要威胁,因此需要发现新的 抗病毒药物 制剂。在这项研究中,针对关键 IAV 蛋白,包括血凝素(H3 和 H5)、神经氨酸酶(N1 和 N2)和 RNA 聚合酶亚基(PB2 和 PA),对 1288 种蓝藻次生代谢物进行了基于结构的虚拟筛选。使用 AutoDock Vina 和刚性受体-柔性配体方法进行分子对接,并根据对接分数、分子大小、结合方向以及与功能关键残基的相互作用来评估结合构象。多目标对接策略与多个对接相关标准相结合,确定了几种有前途的候选物,其预测对接分数比 H3、H5、N1 和 N2 的相应天然配体更有利,但 PB2 和 PA 的分数稍差。主要化合物包括 11,12-二脱氢螺菌酸、7-甲酰基-3-甲氧基-5-甲基茚满酮、菌孢素-甘氨酸-丙氨酸、palythine-丝氨酸、Asterina-330、丰加霉素、usujirene、tjipanaz 衍生物、结核菌素和去甲酰胺 A。值得注意的是,在优先化合物中,来自 Calothrix 的代谢物表现出预测的多靶点结合谱最广泛,其次是 Tolypothrix,而源自 Lyngbya/Moorea、Scytonema、Leptolyngbya、Nostoc 和 Fischerella 的化合物则表现出靶点特异性或有限的多靶点结合谱。基于支架的分析揭示了关键的结构类别,包括核苷样化合物、菌孢素样氨基酸和肽/生物碱衍生物,有助于预测与功能关键残基的良好预测结合相互作用。ADMET 评估揭示了优先化合物中不同的预测药代动力学和毒性特征,其中几种候选化合物表现出预测肠道吸收低或致突变风险升高,这突出表明需要进一步进行安全性和药代动力学评估。总体而言,这项研究强调蓝藻代谢物是一种结构多样且尚未充分开发的抗 IAV 潜在多靶点 抗病毒药物 候选物的来源,并为未来计算细化和实验验证在人类和动物健康方面的应用提供了框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 仍然是全球健康和畜牧业的主要威胁,因此需要发现新的 抗病毒药物 制剂。
设计与对象
在这项研究中,针对关键 IAV 蛋白,包括血凝素(H3 和 H5)、神经氨酸酶(N1 和 N2)和 RNA 聚合酶亚基(PB2 和 PA),对 1288 种蓝藻次生代谢物进行了基于结构的虚拟筛选。
核心方法
使用 AutoDock Vina 和刚性受体-柔性配体方法进行分子对接,并根据对接分数、分子大小、结合方向以及与功能关键残基的相互作用来评估结合构象。
主要结果
多目标对接策略与多个对接相关标准相结合,确定了几种有前途的候选物,其预测对接分数比 H3、H5、N1 和 N2 的相应天然配体更有利,但 PB2 和 PA 的分数稍差。
解释与创新
基于支架的分析揭示了关键的结构类别,包括核苷样化合物、菌孢素样氨基酸和肽/生物碱衍生物,有助于预测与功能关键残基的良好预测结合相互作用。
科研与公卫意义
ADMET 评估揭示了优先化合物中不同的预测药代动力学和毒性特征,其中几种候选化合物表现出预测肠道吸收低或致突变风险升高,这突出表明需要进一步进行安全性和药代动力学评估。

Structure-based virtual screening of cyanobacterial metabolites against key influenza A virus targets

Authors: Mohanakrishnan Neelakrishnan, Brahmaiah Pendyala, Akhil Rautela, Vivek Grandhe, Ankit Patras, Mohana Krishnan Neelakrishnan
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A virus (IAV) remains a major threat to global health and livestock industries, necessitating the discovery of new antiviral agents. In this study, a structure-based virtual screening of 1288 cyanobacterial secondary metabolites was performed against key IAV proteins, including hemagglutinin (H3 and H5), neuraminidase (N1 and N2), and RNA polymerase subunits (PB2 and PA). Molecular docking was conducted using AutoDock Vina with a rigid receptor–flexible ligand approach, and binding conformations were evaluated based on docking scores, molecular size, binding orientation, and interactions with functionally critical residues. A multi-target docking strategy combined with multiple docking-related criteria identified several promising candidates with more favorable predicted docking scores than the corresponding native ligands for H3, H5, N1, and N2, but slightly less favorable scores for PB2 and PA. Key compounds included 11,12-didehydrospironostoic acid, 7-formyl-3-methoxy-5-methylindanone, mycosporine-glycine-alanine, palythine-serine, Asterina-330, toyocamycin, usujirene, tjipanazole derivatives, tubercidin, and didmolamide A. Notably, among the prioritized compounds, metabolites from Calothrix exhibited the broadest predicted multi-target binding profile, followed by Tolypothrix , while compounds derived from Lyngbya/Moorea , Scytonema , Leptolyngbya , Nostoc , and Fischerella demonstrated target-specific or limited multi-target binding profiles. Scaffold-based analysis revealed key structural classes, including nucleoside-like compounds, mycosporine-like amino acids, and peptide/alkaloid derivatives, contributing to favorable predicted binding interactions with functionally critical residues. ADMET evaluation revealed variable predicted pharmacokinetic and toxicity profiles among the prioritized compounds, with several candidates exhibiting low predicted intestinal absorption or elevated mutagenicity risk, highlighting the need for further safety and pharmacokinetic evaluation. Overall, this study highlights cyanobacterial metabolites as a structurally diverse and underexplored source of potential multi-target antiviral candidates against IAV and provides a framework for future computational refinement and experimental validation toward applications in both human and animal health.

通过最近 疫苗 靶标的预先存在的抗体特征来预测针对漂移的甲型流感 (H3N2) 亚支 K 的交叉反应

Cross-reactivity against drifted influenza A (H3N2) subclade K is predicted by pre-existing antibody signatures from recent vaccine targets
作者： Qixin Wang, Lindsay McManus, Daniel Morreale, Samreen Fatima, Ross Blanc, Liping Wang, Hadar Malca, Brian Joughin, Douglas Lauffenburger, Ai-ris Collier
摘要中文翻译摘要 季节性流感 疫苗 菌株是在流感季节开始前几个月选择的,存在 疫苗 不匹配的风险。为此,快速预测针对新出现的流感病毒株的保护是公共卫生准备的需要。此外,了解针对错配靶标的保护的免疫学相关性对于改进年度流感 疫苗接种 组合物和/或平台是必要的。使用改进的系统预测中和抗体(SNAb)方法,我们量化了 2025/2026 流感季节期间人类 队列研究 在 队列研究 前后的预测流感病毒中和反应,在此期间,疫苗 不匹配的 H3N2 亚支 K 占大多数流感病毒感染。这是使用前 10 年的 疫苗 目标完成的,包括 2025/2026 疫苗接种 的组成部分以及 2026/2027 疫苗 目标。我们确定,H3N2 亚支 K 反应者是通过基线 SNAb 谱预测的,特别是 2024/2025 和 2025/2026 流感 疫苗 的 H3N2 成分。系统血清学分析在多变量水平上支持了这一点,在预测 疫苗接种 前体液特征的不匹配反应方面具有更高的准确性。最后,我们采用这种方法来预测谁可能是 2026/2027 流感 疫苗 H1 成分的高反应者,并发现选择用于预测强中和反应的抗体特征对于 2025/2026 流感 疫苗接种 的 H1 成分不成比例地富集。我们的结果支持一个模型,在该模型中,基于系统的方法可以在流感季节开始之前识别预测强烈流感 疫苗接种 反应的特征。

该研究发现,H3N2 亚支 K 反应者是通过基线 SNAb 谱预测的,特别是 2024/2025 和 2025/2026 流感 疫苗 的 H3N2 成分。

Cross-reactivity against drifted influenza A (H3N2) subclade K is predicted by pre-existing antibody signatures from recent vaccine targets

Authors: Qixin Wang, Lindsay McManus, Daniel Morreale, Samreen Fatima, Ross Blanc, Liping Wang, Hadar Malca, Brian Joughin, Douglas Lauffenburger, Ai-ris Collier
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Seasonal influenza vaccine strains are selected months before the flu season begins, risking a vaccine mismatch. To that end, quickly predicting protection against newly emerged influenza strains is a public health preparedness need. Further, understanding immunological correlates of protection against mismatched targets is necessary for improving annual influenza vaccination compositions and/or platforms. Using a modified systems-predicted neutralizing antibody (SNAb) approach, we quantified predicted influenza virus neutralization responses in a human cohort pre- and post-vaccination during the 2025/2026 influenza season, during which the vaccine-mismatched H3N2 subclade K accounted for most influenza virus infections. This was done using the previous 10 years of vaccine targets, including the components of the 2025/2026 vaccination, as well as the 2026/2027 vaccine targets. We identified that H3N2 subclade K responders were predicted by baseline SNAb profiles, particularly to the H3N2 components of the 2024/2025 and 2025/2026 influenza vaccine. Systems serological analysis supported this at the multivariate level, with even greater accuracy in predicting mismatched responses from pre-vaccination humoral signatures. Lastly, we employed this approach to predict who would likely be high responders to the 2026/2027 influenza vaccine H1 component, and found that antibody features selected as predicting a strong neutralization response were disproportionately enriched for the H1 component of the 2025/2026 influenza vaccination. Our results support a model in which systems-based approaches can identify signatures predicting strong influenza vaccination responses before the influenza season begins.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Seasonal influenza vaccine strains are selected months before the flu season begins, risking a vaccine mismatch. This study aimed to address the need for predicting protection against newly emerged influenza strains.
Design and population
The study used a modified systems-predicted neutralizing antibody (SNAb) approach to quantify predicted influenza virus neutralization responses in a human cohort pre- and post-vaccination during the 2025/2026 influenza season, when the vaccine-mismatched H3N2 subclade K accounted for most influenza virus infections.
Core methods
Using a modified systems-predicted neutralizing antibody (SNAb) approach, the researchers quantified predicted influenza virus neutralization responses in a human cohort before and after vaccination during the 2025/2026 influenza season, which was dominated by the vaccine-mismatched H3N2 subclade K.
Main results
The study identified that H3N2 subclade K responders were predicted by baseline SNAb profiles, particularly to the H3N2 components of the 2024/2025 and 2025/2026 influenza vaccine.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that systems-based serological analysis can predict mismatched responses from pre-vaccination humoral signatures, supporting a model for identifying vaccination response signatures before the influenza season begins.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results inform the development of systems-based approaches to improve influenza vaccine composition and predict individual responses to mismatched vaccine targets, enhancing public health preparedness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is limited by its reliance on a single influenza season's data and the potential for unmeasured confounding factors, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. The evidence strength is moderate.

S100A4 通过与人肺泡上皮细胞中的 M2 相互作用促进甲型流感病毒复制

S100A4 facilitates influenza A virus replication by interacting with M2 in human alveolar epithelial cells
作者： Li Wu, Yingying Tao, Jianing Li, Rui Yang, Jiasheng Song, Jianhong Shu, Yulong He, Huapeng Feng
摘要中文翻译甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 复制依赖于 宿主 细胞,并受到众多 宿主 因子的调节。因此,调查和理解这些 宿主 因素的作用是一个重要的课题。在本研究中,我们发现通过RNAi或CRISPR/Cas9基因编辑技术敲除宿主因子S100钙结合蛋白A4(S100A4)后,IAV复制减弱。相反,IAV 复制在 A549 细胞中过度表达后增强。通过免疫共沉淀证实了 S100A4 和 IAV M2 蛋白之间的相互作用。同时,共聚焦显微镜实验表明,在IAV感染的A549细胞中,病毒M2蛋白与内源性S100A4共定位。截短分析证实M2蛋白的氨基酸45-62构成与S100A4的主要相互作用区域。进一步的研究表明,S100A4 缺失后,病毒进入减少。与野生型A549细胞相比,S100A4敲除A549细胞中聚合酶活性显着降低。S100A4过表达导致IFN-β下调,而干扰素刺激基因ISG15和BST2的mRNA水平也显着降低。因此,宿主因子S100A4是IAV复制的正调节剂,并参与病毒进入、聚合酶活性维持和先天免疫调节。总的来说,我们的研究结果为控制 IAV 感染的分子机制提供了新的线索,并提出了一条先前未充分探索的 抗病毒药物 治疗干预途径。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 复制依赖于 宿主 细胞,并受到众多 宿主 因子的调节。因此,调查和理解这些 宿主 因素的作用是一个重要的课题。
设计与对象
在这项研究中,研究人员使用人类肺泡上皮细胞,特别是 A549 细胞,研究 S100A4 在 IAV 复制中的作用。该研究涉及通过RNAi敲低S100A4或通过CRISPR/Cas9基因编辑技术敲除S100A4,并在A549细胞中过度表达它。
核心方法
该研究采用RNAi和CRISPR/Cas9基因编辑技术来敲低或敲除S100A4。使用免疫共沉淀来确认 S100A4 和 IAV M2 蛋白之间的相互作用。利用共聚焦显微镜来证明病毒 M2 蛋白与内源性 S100A4 在 IAV 感染的 A549 细胞中的共定位。
主要结果
在用 RNAi 敲低 S100A4 或用 CRISPR/Cas9 敲除 S100A4 后,IAV 复制减弱。相反,IAV 复制在 A549 细胞中过表达时增强。进一步的研究表明,S100A4 缺失后,病毒进入减少。与野生型A549细胞相比,S100A4敲除A549细胞中的聚合酶活性显着降低。
解释与创新
通过免疫共沉淀证实了 S100A4 和 IAV M2 蛋白之间的相互作用。研究结果表明,S100A4 是 IAV 复制的正调节剂,并参与病毒进入、聚合酶活性维持和先天免疫调节。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为控制 IAV 感染的分子机制提供了新的线索,并提出了一条先前未充分探索的 抗病毒药物 治疗干预途径。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在人类肺泡上皮细胞中进行的,这可能会限制对其他 宿主 系统的推广。该证据基于 S100A4 的体外实验操作,可能无法完全反映体内条件。证据强度中等。

S100A4 facilitates influenza A virus replication by interacting with M2 in human alveolar epithelial cells

Authors: Li Wu, Yingying Tao, Jianing Li, Rui Yang, Jiasheng Song, Jianhong Shu, Yulong He, Huapeng Feng
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A virus (IAV) replication depends on host cells and is regulated by numerous host factors. Therefore, investigating and understanding the role of these host factors is a significant topic. In this study, we found the IAV replication was attenuated after host factor S100 calcium-binding protein A4 (S100A4) was knocked down with RNAi or knockout with CRISPR/Cas9 gene-editing technology. Conversely, IAV replication was enhanced upon the overexpression in A549 cells. The interaction between S100A4 and the IAV M2 protein was confirmed by co-immunoprecipitation. Meanwhile, confocal microscopy experiment demonstrated that the viral M2 protein co-localized with endogenous S100A4 in IAV-infected A549 cells. Truncation analysis confirmed that amino acids 45–62 of the M2 protein constitute the main interaction region with S100A4. Further study showed that viral entry was reduced after S100A4 deletion. And the polymerase activity was significantly decreased in S100A4-knockout A549 cells compared to that in wild-type A549 cells. The S100A4 overexpression resulted in the downregulation of IFN-β, while the mRNA levels of the interferon-stimulated genes ISG15 and BST2 were also significantly reduced. Therefore, the host factor S100A4 was a positive modulator of IAV replication and was involved in the viral entry, polymerase activity maintenance and innate immune regulation. Collectively, our findings shed new light on the molecular mechanisms governing IAV infection and suggest a previously underexplored avenue for antiviral therapeutic intervention.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza A virus (IAV) replication depends on host cells and is regulated by numerous host factors. Therefore, investigating and understanding the role of these host factors is a significant topic.
Design and population
In this study, the researchers used human alveolar epithelial cells, specifically A549 cells, to investigate the role of S100A4 in IAV replication. The study involved knocking down S100A4 with RNAi or knockout with CRISPR/Cas9 gene-editing technology and overexpressing it in A549 cells.
Core methods
The study employed RNAi and CRISPR/Cas9 gene-editing technology to knock down or knockout S100A4. Co-immunoprecipitation was used to confirm the interaction between S100A4 and the IAV M2 protein. Confocal microscopy was utilized to demonstrate co-localization of the viral M2 protein with endogenous S100A4 in IAV-infected A549 cells.
Main results
IAV replication was attenuated after S100A4 was knocked down with RNAi or knockout with CRISPR/Cas9. Conversely, IAV replication was enhanced upon overexpression in A549 cells. Further study showed that viral entry was reduced after S100A4 deletion. The polymerase activity was significantly decreased in S100A4-knockout A549 cells compared to that in wild-type A549 cells.
Interpretation and novelty
The interaction between S100A4 and the IAV M2 protein was confirmed by co-immunoprecipitation. The findings suggest that S100A4 is a positive modulator of IAV replication and is involved in viral entry, polymerase activity maintenance and innate immune regulation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings shed new light on the molecular mechanisms governing IAV infection and suggest a previously underexplored avenue for antiviral therapeutic intervention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in human alveolar epithelial cells, which may limit generalizability to other host systems. The evidence is based on experimental manipulation of S100A4 in vitro, which may not fully reflect in vivo conditions. The evidence strength is moderate.

调节甲型流感病毒片段 7 差异 mRNA 剪接的顺式作用元件的鉴定

Identification of cis-Acting Elements that Regulate Influenza A Virus Segment 7 Differential mRNA Splicing
作者： Zhou, S., Pinto, R. M., Chook, H. W., Lee, H. M., Gaunt, E. R., Digard, P.
摘要中文翻译甲型流感病毒片段 7 分别编码来自未剪接初级转录物 (mRNA1) 和剪接 mRNA2 的 M1 基质蛋白和 M2 离子通道。这两种蛋白对于有效的病毒复制都是必需的,但决定片段 7 剪接平衡的内在序列尚未完全确定。在这里,我们系统地研究了保守的顺式作用剪接元件如何调节片段剪接。我们发现,虽然剪接受体序列是最佳的,但两个潜在的上游多嘧啶束都调节剪接,5'区域起正作用,3'区域起负作用以维持M1表达。核苷酸 A713 是最有可能的 mRNA2 分支点,但可能与其他局部腺苷存在冗余。mRNA2 剪接供体序列的诱变表明它几乎是最佳的,但核苷酸 51 和 55 适度下调剪接效率。大多数引入的改变拼接平衡的突变在M1中都是非同义的。然而,显着扰乱 M1:M2 平衡的多嘧啶束和分支点 突变 产生了存活的病毒。相反,剪接供体序列很大程度上不耐受 突变。我们得出的结论是,M1:M2 平衡是通过次优剪接供体和聚嘧啶束剪接信号来维持的,后者允许更大的进化灵活性来改变比率和/或 M1 序列
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在鉴定调节甲型流感病毒片段 7 差异 mRNA 剪接的顺式作用元件,该片段分别编码来自未剪接和剪接转录本的 M1 基质蛋白和 M2 离子通道。这项研究的动机是需要了解决定这两种蛋白质之间剪接平衡的内在序列。
设计与对象
该研究系统地研究了甲型流感病毒中保守的顺式作用剪接元件如何调节第 7 段剪接。它侧重于 M1 和 M2 表达之间的平衡,没有具体提及研究日期、中心或样本量。
核心方法
研究人员对 mRNA2 剪接供体序列进行了诱变,并分析了对剪接效率的影响。他们还研究了潜在的上游多嘧啶束和分支点在调节剪接平衡中的作用。
主要结果
mRNA2 剪接供体序列的诱变表明它几乎是最佳的,但核苷酸 51 和 55 适度下调剪接效率。此外,多嘧啶束的 5' 区域起正作用,而 3' 区域起负作用以维持 M1 表达。
解释与创新
作者解释说,M1:M2 平衡是通过次优剪接供体和多嘧啶束剪接信号来维持的,后者提供了更多的进化灵活性来改变比率和/或 M1 序列。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了对甲型流感病毒中调节 mRNA 剪接的分子机制的见解,这可以为未来病毒基因表达的研究提供信息,并可能有助于开发靶向 抗病毒药物 策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象级别的证据,不包括详细的实验结果或统计分析。证据强度中等,因为结论是基于诱变和剪接信号分析而没有进一步验证。

Identification of cis-Acting Elements that Regulate Influenza A Virus Segment 7 Differential mRNA Splicing

Authors: Zhou, S., Pinto, R. M., Chook, H. W., Lee, H. M., Gaunt, E. R., Digard, P.
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A virus segment 7 encodes the M1 matrix protein and M2 ion channel from an unspliced primary transcript (mRNA1) and spliced mRNA2, respectively. Both proteins are essential for efficient virus replication, but the intrinsic sequences that determine segment 7 splicing balance are not fully defined. Here, we systematically investigated how conserved cis-acting splicing elements regulate segment splicing. We found that while the splice acceptor sequence is optimal, both potential upstream polypyrimidine tracts regulate splicing, with the 5' region acting positively and the 3' region negatively to maintain M1 expression. Nucleotide A713 is the most likely mRNA2 branch point, but potentially with redundancy from other local adenosines. Mutagenesis of the mRNA2 splice donor sequence showed that it is nearly optimal but that nucleotides 51 and 55 modestly downregulate splicing efficiency. Most introduced mutations that change splicing balance are nonsynonymous in M1. Nevertheless, polypyrimidine tract and branch point mutations that significantly perturbed M1:M2 balance resulted in viable viruses. In contrast, the splice donor sequence was largely intolerant of mutation. We conclude that M1:M2 balance is maintained via suboptimal splice donor and polypyrimidine tract splicing signals, with the latter allowing more evolutionary flexibility to vary the ratio and/or M1 sequence
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify cis-acting elements that regulate differential mRNA splicing of influenza A virus segment 7, which encodes the M1 matrix protein and M2 ion channel from unspliced and spliced transcripts, respectively. The research was motivated by the need to understand the intrinsic sequences that determine the splicing balance between these two proteins.
Design and population
The study was a systematic investigation of how conserved cis-acting splicing elements regulate segment 7 splicing in influenza A virus. It focused on the balance between M1 and M2 expression, with no specific mention of study dates, centres, or sample size.
Core methods
The researchers performed mutagenesis of the mRNA2 splice donor sequence and analyzed the effects on splicing efficiency. They also examined the roles of potential upstream polypyrimidine tracts and the branch point in regulating splicing balance.
Main results
Mutagenesis of the mRNA2 splice donor sequence showed that it is nearly optimal, but nucleotides 51 and 55 modestly downregulate splicing efficiency. Additionally, the 5' region of the polypyrimidine tract acts positively, while the 3' region acts negatively to maintain M1 expression.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the M1:M2 balance is maintained through suboptimal splice donor and polypyrimidine tract splicing signals, with the latter providing more evolutionary flexibility to vary the ratio and/or M1 sequence.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the molecular mechanisms that regulate mRNA splicing in influenza A virus, which could inform future studies on viral gene expression and potentially contribute to the development of targeted antiviral strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level evidence and does not include detailed experimental results or statistical analyses. The evidence strength is moderate as the conclusions are based on mutagenesis and splicing signal analysis without further validation.

乙型流感循环和 疫苗 对就医门诊乙型流感疾病的有效性,美国,2016-17 年至 2024-25 年。

Influenza B Circulation and Vaccine Effectiveness Against Medically Attended Outpatient Influenza B Illness, United States, 2016-17 Through 2024-25.
作者： Jessica R Meeker, Ashley M. Price, Emily Toth Martin, Ivana A. Vaughn, Karen J. Wernli, C. Hallie Phillips, Manjusha J Gaglani, Chandni Raiyani, Huong Q. Nguyen, Jennifer P. King
摘要中文翻译背景:流感B病毒株的疫苗有效性(VE)在不同季节、谱系、抗原漂移和疫苗组成方面有所不同。在B/Yamagata病毒株全球检测停止后,含有B/Victoria病毒株的三价疫苗于2024-25赛季重新引入。我们评估了流感B病毒的传播情况以及在这一转变期间的八个分析季节中的疫苗有效性。方法:在2016-17至2024-25年的八个流感季节中,美国流感疫苗有效性网络站点招募了年龄≥6个月且患有急性呼吸道疾病的门诊患者;由于2020-21年流感传播量极少,该年份被排除。参与者通过RT-PCR进行检测。使用测试阴性设计和调整年龄、性别、种族/民族、日历时间和研究地点的逻辑回归分析,估计了特定谱系的疫苗有效性。对于流感B病例≥50的季节-谱系分层,报告了疫苗有效性结果。结果:在65,773名参与者中,有3,629例流感B病例纳入谱系特异性疫苗有效性分析(1,507例B/Yamagata;2,122例B/Victoria)。10个季节-谱系分层中有6个达到报告阈值。2016-17年B/Victoria病毒株的疫苗有效性为58%(95%置信区间,28%-77%),2019-20年为51%(43%-57%),2023-24年为78%(71%-84%),2024-25年为39%(14%-56%)。2016-17年和2017-18年B/Yamagata病毒株的疫苗有效性分别为55%(46%-63%)和49%(40%-56%)。结论:流感疫苗对需要医疗干预的流感B门诊病例提供了保护,尽管疫苗有效性在不同季节有所变化。需要持续监测疫苗有效性并进行遗传监测,以检测新兴的流感B病毒株并为疫苗株选择提供信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
B 型流感 疫苗 有效性 (VE) 因季节、谱系、抗原漂移 和 疫苗 成分而异。B/Yamagata 在全球范围内停止检测后,含有 B/Victoria 的三价 疫苗 于 2024-25 年重新引入。
设计与对象
我们评估了跨越这一转变的八个分析季节的乙型流感循环和 VE。在 2016-17 年至 2024-25 年的八个流感季节期间,美国流感 VE 网络站点招募了年龄≥6 个月的患有急性呼吸道疾病的门诊患者;2020-21 年被排除在外,因为流感传播量可以忽略不计。
核心方法
参与者接受了 RT-PCR 测试。使用测试阴性设计和针对年龄、性别、种族/民族、日历时间和研究地点进行调整的逻辑回归来估计谱系特异性 VE。
主要结果
VE 报告为≥50 例乙型流感病例的季节谱系层。在 65,773 名参与者中,3,629 例乙型流感病例被纳入谱系特异性 VE 分析(1,507 例 B/山形;2,122 例 B/维多利亚)。10 个季节谱系阶层中有 6 个达到了报告门槛。
解释与创新
尽管 VE 随季节变化,但流感 疫苗 可以预防门诊 B 型流感疾病。
科研与公卫意义
需要持续的 VE 监测和遗传 监测 来检测新出现的乙型流感病毒并为 疫苗 毒株选择提供信息。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性。

Influenza B Circulation and Vaccine Effectiveness Against Medically Attended Outpatient Influenza B Illness, United States, 2016-17 Through 2024-25.

Authors: Jessica R Meeker, Ashley M. Price, Emily Toth Martin, Ivana A. Vaughn, Karen J. Wernli, C. Hallie Phillips, Manjusha J Gaglani, Chandni Raiyani, Huong Q. Nguyen, Jennifer P. King
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Influenza B vaccine effectiveness (VE) has varied by season, lineage, antigenic drift, and vaccine composition. After B/Yamagata detection ceased globally, trivalent vaccines containing B/Victoria were reintroduced in 2024-25. We evaluated influenza B circulation and VE across eight analyzed seasons spanning this transition. METHODS: Ambulatory patients aged ≥6 months with acute respiratory illness were enrolled at U.S. Flu VE Network sites during eight influenza seasons from 2016-17 through 2024-25; 2020-21 was excluded because influenza circulation was negligible. Participants were tested by RT-PCR. Lineage-specific VE was estimated using a test-negative design and logistic regression adjusted for age, sex, race/ethnicity, calendar time, and study site. VE was reported for season-lineage strata with ≥50 influenza B cases. RESULTS: Among 65,773 participants, 3,629 influenza B cases were included in lineage-specific VE analyses (1,507 B/Yamagata; 2,122 B/Victoria). Six of 10 season-lineage strata met the reporting threshold. B/Victoria VE was 58% (95% CI, 28%-77%) in 2016-17, 51% (43%-57%) in 2019-20, 78% (71%-84%) in 2023-24, and 39% (14%-56%) in 2024-25. B/Yamagata VE was 55% (46%-63%) in 2016-17 and 49% (40%-56%) in 2017-18. CONCLUSIONS: Influenza vaccines protected against medically attended outpatient influenza B illness, although VE varied by season. Continued VE monitoring and genetic surveillance are needed to detect emerging influenza B viruses and inform vaccine strain selection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza B vaccine effectiveness (VE) has varied by season, lineage, antigenic drift, and vaccine composition. After B/Yamagata detection ceased globally, trivalent vaccines containing B/Victoria were reintroduced in 2024-25.

Core methods
Participants were tested by RT-PCR. Lineage-specific VE was estimated using a test-negative design and logistic regression adjusted for age, sex, race/ethnicity, calendar time, and study site.
Main results

Interpretation and novelty
Influenza vaccines protected against medically attended outpatient influenza B illness, although VE varied by season.
Scientific and public-health significance
Continued VE monitoring and genetic surveillance are needed to detect emerging influenza B viruses and inform vaccine strain selection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design.

更多射门:异源流感 Prime-Boost 疫苗接种 降低了对当前或历史变异的抗体反应可忽略不计的可能性

More Shots on Goal: Heterologous Influenza Prime-Boost Vaccination Decreases the Likelihood of a Negligible Antibody Response to Current or Historical Variants
作者： Sierra Orsinelli-Rivers, Ted M. Ross, Tal Einav, Ross, T. M.
摘要中文翻译季节性流感 疫苗接种 仅在一部分受者中引起强烈且持久的抗体反应,之前的同源加强试验未能改善单剂量 疫苗接种。在这里,我们进行了一项 4 期临床试验,以测试异源初免-加强是否会增加抗体的强度或宽度以及降低无反应率(ClinicalTrials.gov ID# NCT06567860)。参与者接受了 2023 年末季灭活流感 疫苗,四个月后又接受了 2024 年加强疫苗,其中包含更新的 H3N2 毒株以及未改变的 H1N1 和 B/Victoria 毒株,从而可以在受试者内比较异源和同源加强疫苗。加强剂 疫苗接种 停止或逆转了所有 疫苗 菌株的抗体滴度减弱,最显着的是恢复了 B/Victoria 的 队列研究 水平的血清保护和血清转换滴度。至关重要的是,异源加强剂降低了无反应者的比例(在所有时间点均表现出 HAI[≤]10),接受初免和加强剂的受试者中 H3N2 无反应者为 0%,而 H1N1 无反应者为 29%。在连续秋季服用 队列研究 的较大 队列研究 中也存在类似的模式,其中 H3N2 的无反应率为 20%,而 H1N1 的无反应率为 37%。在所有对 疫苗 菌株滴度始终较低的无应答者中,多达一半引发了针对历史变体的抗体应答,这表明免疫印记部分是无应答者表型的基础。尽管 疫苗接种 时间不同,但当 队列研究 与 疫苗接种 前滴度匹配时,季末启动和标准秋季 疫苗接种 在 疫苗接种 后 30 天产生了相当的抗体宽度。这些发现表明,异源 疫苗接种 或其他多毒株暴露可以减少在流感季节期间未产生抗体反应的人群比例。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
季节性流感 疫苗接种 仅在一部分受者中引起强烈且持久的抗体反应,之前的同源加强试验未能改善单剂量 疫苗接种。
设计与对象
在这里,我们进行了一项 4 期临床试验,以测试异源初免-加强是否会增加抗体的强度或宽度以及降低无反应率(ClinicalTrials.gov ID# NCT06567860)。
核心方法
参与者接受了 2023 年末季灭活流感 疫苗,四个月后又接受了 2024 年加强疫苗,其中包含更新的 H3N2 毒株以及未改变的 H1N1 和 B/Victoria 毒株,从而可以在受试者内比较异源和同源加强疫苗。
主要结果
至关重要的是,异源加强剂降低了无反应者的比例(在所有时间点均表现出 HAI[≤]10),接受初免和加强剂的受试者中 H3N2 无反应者为 0%,而 H1N1 无反应者为 29%。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,异源 疫苗接种 或其他多毒株暴露可以减少在流感季节期间未产生抗体反应的人群比例。
科研与公卫意义
尽管 疫苗接种 时间不同,但当 队列研究 与 疫苗接种 前滴度匹配时,季末启动和标准秋季 疫苗接种 在 疫苗接种 后 30 天产生了相当的抗体宽度。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告异源初免-加强策略的长期疗效或安全性。

More Shots on Goal: Heterologous Influenza Prime-Boost Vaccination Decreases the Likelihood of a Negligible Antibody Response to Current or Historical Variants

Authors: Sierra Orsinelli-Rivers, Ted M. Ross, Tal Einav, Ross, T. M.
Original abstract (English)
Seasonal influenza vaccination only elicits a strong and durable antibody response in a subset of recipients, and prior homologous booster trials have failed to improve on single-dose vaccination. Here, we conducted a phase 4 clinical trial to test if a heterologous prime-boost increases antibody magnitude or breadth as well as reduces the rate of non-responders (ClinicalTrials.gov ID# NCT06567860). Participants received a late-season 2023 inactivated influenza vaccine followed by the 2024 booster four months later containing an updated H3N2 strain alongside unchanged H1N1 and B/Victoria strains, allowing within-subject comparison of heterologous and homologous boosting. The booster vaccination halted or reversed antibody titer waning for all vaccine strains, most notably restoring seroprotective and seroconverting titers for B/Victoria at the cohort-level. Critically, the heterologous booster decreased the rate of non-responders (exhibiting HAI[≤]10 at all time points), with 0% H3N2 non-responders among subjects receiving the prime and boost compared to 29% H1N1 non-responders. A similar pattern held in a larger cohort administering consecutive fall vaccinations, where non-response rate was 20% of H3N2 compared to 37% for H1N1. Across all non-responders that had consistently low titers against the vaccine strains, up to half elicited an antibody response against historical variants, suggesting that immune imprinting partially underlies the non-responder phenotype. Despite differing vaccination timing, late-season priming and standard fall vaccination produced comparable antibody breadth 30 days post-vaccination when cohorts were matched on pre-vaccination titers. These findings suggest that heterologous vaccinations or other multi-strain exposures can reduce the fraction of the population that does not mount an antibody response during an influenza season.
View research seven-element analysis

Design and population

Core methods

Main results
Critically, the heterologous booster decreased the rate of non-responders (exhibiting HAI[≤]10 at all time points), with 0% H3N2 non-responders among subjects receiving the prime and boost compared to 29% H1N1 non-responders.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on the long-term efficacy or safety of the heterologous prime-boost strategy.

序列结构的适应度景观预测流感 进化

Sequence-structured fitness landscapes predict influenza evolution
作者： Edwin Rodríguez-Horta, John P. Barton
摘要中文翻译季节性流感 病毒不断进化以逃避 宿主 免疫。疫苗 必须定期更新,以防范新的流行毒株,从而激励人们努力预测病毒 进化。最近的实验和计算方法在这个问题上取得了进展,但学习驱动病毒 进化 的适应度景观仍然具有挑战性,因为 基因组监测 只采样了可能序列空间的一小部分。在这里,我们开发了一个生成框架,该框架可以共享遗传相关变体的信息,从时间序列数据中推断出对病毒适应性的有效 突变 级别的贡献。应用于 A/H3N2 和 A/H1N1 病毒的血凝素和神经氨酸酶,推断的适应度景观恢复了已知的抗原性决定因素,并揭示了与常见抗体介导的选择一致的跨亚型的趋同空间组织。来自这些景观的正向模拟预测了循环 突变 的固定或丢失以及病毒种群的未来组成,优于基于中性 进化 或多项 Logistic 增长的模型。总的来说,我们的结果表明,病原体适应度景观的可解释表示可以揭示生物学上的重要特征并改善前瞻性进化预测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过开发一个生成框架来解决预测流感 进化 的挑战,该框架从序列数据推断适应度景观,其动机是需要针对新的流行毒株更新 疫苗。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用时间序列数据开发适用于 A/H3N2 和 A/H1N1 病毒的血凝素和神经氨酸酶的生成框架。
核心方法
该研究利用生成框架来共享遗传相关变体的信息,从序列数据中推断出 突变 级别对病毒适应性的贡献。
主要结果
推断的适应度景观恢复了已知的抗原性决定因素,并揭示了跨亚型的趋同空间组织,正向模拟预测了循环 突变 的固定或丢失以及病毒种群的未来组成,优于中性 进化 和多项 Logistic 增长模型。
解释与创新
结果表明,病原体适应度景观的可解释表示可以揭示生物学上的重要特征并改善前瞻性进化预测。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 监测、疫苗 的开发和进化预测提供信息,从而增强预测和应对新出现的流感病毒株的能力。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括从一小部分序列空间学习适应度景观的挑战以及需要使用更广泛的数据集进行进一步验证。

Sequence-structured fitness landscapes predict influenza evolution

Authors: Edwin Rodríguez-Horta, John P. Barton
Original abstract (English)
Seasonal influenza viruses continually evolve to evade host immunity. Vaccines must be regularly updated to protect against new circulating strains, motivating efforts to forecast viral evolution. Recent experimental and computational methods have made progress on this problem, but learning the fitness landscape driving viral evolution remains challenging because genomic surveillance samples only a small fraction of the possible sequence space. Here, we developed a generative framework that shares information across genetically related variants, inferring effective mutation-level contributions to viral fitness from temporal sequence data. Applied to hemagglutinin and neuraminidase from A/H3N2 and A/H1N1 viruses, the inferred fitness landscapes recovers known determinants of antigenicity and reveals convergent spatial organization across subtypes consistent with common antibody-mediated selection. Forward simulations from these landscapes predicted both the fixation or loss of circulating mutations and the future composition of viral populations, outperforming models based on neutral evolution or multinomial-logistic growth. Collectively, our results show that interpretable representations of pathogen fitness landscapes can reveal biologically important features and improve prospective evolutionary forecasting.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of predicting influenza evolution by developing a generative framework that infers fitness landscapes from sequence data, motivated by the need to update vaccines against new circulating strains.
Design and population
The research involved developing a generative framework applied to hemagglutinin and neuraminidase from A/H3N2 and A/H1N1 viruses, using temporal sequence data.
Core methods
The study utilized a generative framework to share information across genetically related variants, inferring mutation-level contributions to viral fitness from sequence data.
Main results
The inferred fitness landscapes recovered known determinants of antigenicity and revealed convergent spatial organization across subtypes, and forward simulations predicted the fixation or loss of circulating mutations and the future composition of viral populations, outperforming neutral evolution and multinomial-logistic growth models.
Interpretation and novelty
The results showed that interpretable representations of pathogen fitness landscapes can reveal biologically important features and improve prospective evolutionary forecasting.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance, vaccine development, and evolutionary forecasting, enhancing the ability to predict and respond to emerging influenza strains.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the challenge of learning fitness landscapes from a small fraction of sequence space and the need for further validation with more extensive datasets.
