COVID-19 大流行后巴基斯坦流感亚型动态的时间变化:2015-2024 年 10 年 FluNet 分析。

Temporal Shifting of Influenza Subtype Dynamics in Pakistan Following the COVID-19 Pandemic: A 10-Year FluNet Analysis, 2015-2024.
作者： Ahmar Jan Qureshi, Muhammad Riyyan Tariq Tagga, Hussnain Ali, Aliza Tariq, Modibo Yattassaye
摘要中文翻译背景:COVID-19 大流行和相关的非药物干预措施导致全球流感传播受到前所未有的抑制。巴基斯坦人口密度高,监测 能力不断发展,缺乏有关 COVID-19 后流感模式的长期数据,而这对于指导 疫苗 政策至关重要。方法:我们从 WHO FluNet 平台(2015 年 1 月 1 日至 2024 年 12 月 31 日)提取了巴基斯坦每周的病毒学数据。研究时期分为COVID-19之前(2015-2019)、COVID-19中断(2020-2021)和COVID-19之后(2022-2024)阶段。结果包括 A 型与 B 型比例、亚型分布(A[H1N1]pdm09 与 A[H3])、阳性率、峰值强度和季节持续时间(阳性率≥ 10% 的周数)。统计比较使用卡方进行亚型分布,使用 Kruskal-Wallis 进行峰值强度。结果:我们分析了 79,484 份检测中的 6522 份阳性样本(10,781 份在 COVID-19 之前;14,687 份在 COVID-19 中断期间;54,016 份在 COVID-19 后)。甲型流感从 94.2% 下降至 71.7%,然后上升至 77.5%;乙型流感从 5.7% 增加至 28.3%,维持在 22.5% (p < 0.001)。A(H3) 在 COVID-19 干扰期间占主导地位 (98.8%);甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 在 COVID-19 后重新占据主导地位 (58.8%),但 A(H3) 仍高于 COVID-19 前(41.2% 与峰值强度不同 (p = 0.047):COVID-19 前 33.3%-67.6%;COVID-19 中断 14.0%-17.6%;以及 COVID-19 后19.8%-37.8%。2020 年季节持续时间为 1 周,2021 年为非连续的 3 周,而 COVID-19 之前为 10-16 周,恢复到 COVID-19 后 7-13 周。结论:COVID-19 大流行导致巴基斯坦流感 流行病学 持续升高,并重塑了 A 亚型平衡。与 B/Yamagata 的全球消失一致,并支持考虑三价 疫苗 制剂,尽管仍需要抗原和遗传数据来确认 疫苗 匹配。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 COVID-19 大流行如何影响巴基斯坦的流感传播,特别关注亚型动态的变化。背景强调,大流行及其干预措施导致全球流感活动受到抑制。
设计与对象
该研究分析了巴基斯坦WHO FluNet平台的每周病毒学数据,涵盖2015年1月1日至2024年12月31日。它将数据分为三个阶段:COVID-19之前(2015-2019年)、COVID-19中断(2020-2021年)和COVID-19之后(2022-2024年)。
核心方法
研究人员提取了病毒学数据并对结果进行了分类,例如亚型分布、阳性率、峰值强度和季节持续时间。他们使用卡方检验进行亚型分布,使用 Kruskal-Wallis 检验进行峰值强度比较。
主要结果
研究发现,甲型流感在大流行期间从94.2%下降到71.7%,然后又上升到77.5%。B 型流感从 5.7% 增加到 28.3%,保持在 22.5% (p < 0.001)。A(H3)在疫情期间占据主导地位,而甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09随后重新占据主导地位。各个阶段的峰值强度各不相同,与大流行前和大流行后相比,大流行期间的季节持续时间较短。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,这场大流行导致巴基斯坦流感 流行病学 发生持久变化,乙型流感增加,并且 A 亚型之间的平衡发生变化。这与 B/Yamagata 消失的全球趋势一致。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,巴基斯坦的 疫苗 政策应考虑流感动态的变化,特别是乙型流感 患病率 的增加以及对三价 疫苗 制剂的需求。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象数据,这可能会限制分析的深度。证据是中等的,因为它依赖于观察趋势,没有详细的机制或因果解释。

Temporal Shifting of Influenza Subtype Dynamics in Pakistan Following the COVID-19 Pandemic: A 10-Year FluNet Analysis, 2015-2024.

Authors: Ahmar Jan Qureshi, Muhammad Riyyan Tariq Tagga, Hussnain Ali, Aliza Tariq, Modibo Yattassaye
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: The COVID-19 pandemic and associated nonpharmaceutical interventions caused unprecedented suppression of influenza circulation globally. Pakistan, with its high population density and evolving surveillance capacity, lacks long-term data on post-COVID-19 influenza patterns, essential for guiding vaccine policy. METHODS: We extracted weekly virological data for Pakistan from the WHO FluNet platform (January 1, 2015, to December 31, 2024). The study period was divided into pre-COVID-19 (2015-2019), COVID-19 disruption (2020-2021), and post-COVID-19 (2022-2024) phases. Outcomes included type A versus B proportion, subtype distribution (A[H1N1]pdm09 vs. A[H3]), positivity, peak intensity, and season duration (weeks with positivity ≥ 10%). Statistical comparisons used chi-square for subtype distribution and Kruskal-Wallis for peak intensity. RESULTS: We analyzed 6522 positive specimens from 79,484 tested (10,781 pre-COVID-19; 14,687 during COVID-19 disruption; 54,016 post-COVID-19). Influenza A declined from 94.2% to 71.7% then rose to 77.5%; influenza B increased from 5.7% to 28.3%, remaining at 22.5% (p < 0.001). A(H3) dominated during the COVID-19 disruption (98.8%); A(H1N1)pdm09 regained dominance post-COVID-19 (58.8%), though A(H3) remained higher than pre-COVID-19 (41.2% vs. Peak intensity differed (p = 0.047): pre-COVID-19 33.3%-67.6%; COVID-19 disruption 14.0%-17.6%; and post-COVID-19 19.8%-37.8%. Season duration was 1 week in 2020 and 3 non-consecutive weeks in 2021, versus 10-16 weeks pre-COVID-19, recovering to 7-13 weeks post-COVID-19. CONCLUSION: The COVID-19 pandemic induced a lasting shift in Pakistan's influenza epidemiology, with persistently elevated influenza B and reshaped A subtype balance. These findings are consistent with the global disappearance of B/Yamagata and support consideration of trivalent vaccine formulations, though antigenic and genetic data remain necessary to confirm vaccine match.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate how the COVID-19 pandemic affected influenza circulation in Pakistan, particularly focusing on the shift in subtype dynamics. The background highlights that the pandemic and its interventions led to a global suppression of influenza activity.
Design and population
The study analyzed weekly virological data from the WHO FluNet platform in Pakistan, covering the period from January 1, 2015, to December 31, 2024. It divided the data into three phases: pre-COVID-19 (2015-2019), COVID-19 disruption (2020-2021), and post-COVID-19 (2022-2024).
Core methods
The researchers extracted virological data and categorized outcomes such as subtype distribution, positivity, peak intensity, and season duration. They used chi-square tests for subtype distribution and Kruskal-Wallis tests for peak intensity comparisons.
Main results
The study found that influenza A declined from 94.2% to 71.7% during the pandemic and then increased to 77.5%. Influenza B increased from 5.7% to 28.3%, remaining at 22.5% (p < 0.001). A(H3) dominated during the pandemic, while A(H1N1)pdm09 regained dominance afterward. Peak intensity varied across phases, and season duration was shorter during the pandemic compared to pre- and post-pandemic periods.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that the pandemic caused a lasting shift in Pakistan's influenza epidemiology, with increased influenza B and changes in the balance between A subtypes. This aligns with global trends of B/Yamagata disappearance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that vaccine policy in Pakistan should consider the altered influenza dynamics, particularly the increased prevalence of influenza B and the need for trivalent vaccine formulations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only data, which may limit the depth of analysis. The evidence is moderate, as it relies on observational trends without detailed mechanistic or causal explanations.

H1N1 流感印记解开小鼠血清学和 B 细胞反应

Influenza H1N1 Imprinting Uncouples Serological and B-Cell Responses in Mice
作者： Elias Tjarnhage, Khang Le Quy, Kasahara TdM, Marit Lovise Toven Daehli, Victor Greiff, Gunnveig Grodeland, Elias Tjärnhage, Khang Lê Quý, Taissa de Matos Kasahara, Gunnveig Grødeland
摘要中文翻译抗原漂移 降低了针对早期流感变种产生的抗体的保护潜力。不幸的是,新的感染或 疫苗接种 通常会优先回忆起针对先前遇到的菌株的抗体,从而限制了对新出现的变体的反应。尽管这种被称为免疫印记的现象在血清学水平上已得到充分记录,但其潜在的细胞机制仍然知之甚少。为了解决血清学和细胞水平上的印记问题,我们将小鼠的血清抗体分析与靶向 B 细胞表征相结合,包括二次 H1N1 攻击后生发中心 (GC) B 细胞的单细胞 B 细胞受体 (BCR) 测序和骨髓浆细胞的批量 BCR 测序。我们发现,2009 年大流行的 H1N1(“猪流感”)毒株的初次感染在很大程度上阻止了继二次接触抗原独特的 H1N1 毒株 A/Puerto Rico/8/1934 (PR8) 后新血清抗体的产生,而相互感染序列更新了针对上次遇到的病毒的抗体特异性。GC B 细胞不反映血清学印记,而是生成针对异源攻击菌株的更新的 BCR 库。同样,引流淋巴结 B 细胞中的 BCR 多样性未能反映抗体反应中观察到的血清学印记。总之,这些发现揭示了流感印记过程中细胞和血清学 免疫应答 的惊人解偶联,强调需要研究这两个区室,以更好地了解免疫记忆并指导在不同人群中改进 季节性流感 疫苗 的开发。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解流感印记过程中细胞和血清学 免疫应答 的解偶联,解决先前血清学研究未完全阐明潜在细胞机制的局限性。
设计与对象
该研究涉及小鼠,首先感染 2009 年大流行的 H1N1 毒株,然后再接受不同 H1N1 毒株的二次攻击,反之亦然,以研究血清学和细胞水平上的印记。
核心方法
使用生发中心 (GC) B 细胞的血清抗体分析和单细胞 B 细胞受体 (BCR) 测序以及骨髓浆细胞的批量 BCR 测序来表征小鼠中的 免疫应答。
主要结果
2009 年大流行的 H1N1 病毒株的初次感染在很大程度上阻止了二次暴露于不同的 H1N1 病毒株后新血清抗体的产生,而引流淋巴结 B 细胞中的 GC B 细胞和 BCR 多样性并未反映血清学印记。
解释与创新
研究结果揭示了流感印记过程中细胞和血清学 免疫应答 的惊人解偶联,强调需要研究这两个区室以更好地了解免疫记忆并指导 疫苗 的发展。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现对于通过了解细胞和血清学 免疫应答 的解偶联来改进 季节性流感 疫苗 具有重要意义,这有助于在不同人群中开发更有效的 疫苗。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括使用小鼠,可能无法完全代表人类的反应,以及关注特定的 H1N1 病毒株,限制了对其他流感病毒株的普遍适用性。

Influenza H1N1 Imprinting Uncouples Serological and B-Cell Responses in Mice

Authors: Elias Tjarnhage, Khang Le Quy, Kasahara TdM, Marit Lovise Toven Daehli, Victor Greiff, Gunnveig Grodeland, Elias Tjärnhage, Khang Lê Quý, Taissa de Matos Kasahara, Gunnveig Grødeland
Original abstract (English)
Antigenic drift reduces the protective potential of antibodies generated against earlier influenza variants. Unfortunately, a new infection or vaccination often preferentially recalls antibodies targeting previously encountered strains, thereby limiting responses to newly emerged variants. Although this phenomenon, known as immunological imprinting, has been well documented at the serological level, its underlying cellular mechanisms remain poorly understood. To resolve imprinting at both the serological and cellular level, we combined serum antibody profiling with targeted B-cell characterization in mice, including single-cell B-cell receptor (BCR) sequencing of germinal center (GC) B cells following secondary H1N1 challenge and bulk BCR sequencing of bone marrow plasma cells. We found that a primary infection with the 2009 pandemic H1N1 ("swine flu") strain largely prevented the generation of new serum antibodies following secondary exposure to the antigenically distinct H1N1 strain A/Puerto Rico/8/1934 (PR8), whereas the reciprocal infection sequence updated antibody specificities towards the last encountered virus. GC B cells did not mirror serological imprinting and instead generated updated BCR repertoires directed against the heterologous challenge strain. Likewise, BCR diversity in draining lymph node B cells failed to mirror the serological imprinting observed in the antibody response. Together, these findings reveal a striking uncoupling of cellular and serological immune responses during influenza imprinting, highlighting the need to study both compartments to better understand immune memory and guide the development of improved seasonal influenza vaccines across a diverse population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the uncoupling of cellular and serological immune responses during influenza imprinting, addressing the limitations of previous serological studies that did not fully elucidate the underlying cellular mechanisms.
Design and population
The study involved mice, with a primary infection with the 2009 pandemic H1N1 strain followed by a secondary challenge with a different H1N1 strain, and vice versa, to investigate imprinting at both serological and cellular levels.
Core methods
Serum antibody profiling and single-cell B-cell receptor (BCR) sequencing of germinal center (GC) B cells and bulk BCR sequencing of bone marrow plasma cells were used to characterize the immune response in mice.
Main results
A primary infection with the 2009 pandemic H1N1 strain largely prevented the generation of new serum antibodies following secondary exposure to a different H1N1 strain, while GC B cells and BCR diversity in draining lymph node B cells did not mirror serological imprinting.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings revealed a striking uncoupling of cellular and serological immune responses during influenza imprinting, highlighting the need to study both compartments to better understand immune memory and guide vaccine development.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings have implications for improving seasonal influenza vaccines by understanding the uncoupling of cellular and serological immune responses, which can help in developing more effective vaccines across a diverse population.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the use of mice, which may not fully represent human responses, and the focus on a specific H1N1 strain, limiting generalizability to other influenza strains.

2024-2026 年希腊南部连续两个季节流感病毒的 基因组监测 和 进化

Genomic Surveillance and Evolution of Influenza Virus Across Two Consecutive Seasons, 2024–2026, in Southern Greece
作者： Konstantina Dragoumani, Georgia Tzouganatou, Maria Evangelidou, Marios Konstantinos Sifakis, Maria Tsintziloni, Emmanouil Angelakis, Mary Emmanouil
摘要中文翻译流感病毒的连续 基因组监测 对于监测病毒 进化、检测新出现的变异并告知 疫苗 毒株选择至关重要。虽然部分基因桑格测序传统上是国家 监测 计划的基础,但全 基因组 测序 (WGS) 可以同时表征所有八个基因组片段,提供通过传统方法无法实现的分辨率。这是希腊报告的第一个发表的、系统的、基于整体 基因组 的流感 监测 研究。我们使用牛津纳米孔技术 (ONT) 测序技术对 2024-2025 年和 2025-2026 年连续两个流感季节在希腊南部 (S. Greek) 传播的流感病毒进行了测序和特征分析。总共分析了 40 个样品,其中绝大多数实现了完整或接近完整的 基因组 组装。2024-2025年期间,通过检测到携带NA的奥司他韦耐药H1N1变种,证实了甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09(亚进化枝C.1.9.3和D.3.1)、甲型H3N2流感病毒(亚进化枝J.1.1和J.2.2)和B/Victoria(C.5亚进化枝,主要是C.5.7)流感病毒的共同传播H275Y 是一项具有临床意义的发现。监测 延伸至 2025-2026 年揭示了 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 从分支 C.1.9.3 到 D.3.1/D.3.1.1 的完整季节间谱系转变,以及 甲型H3N2流感病毒 分支 K 在希腊南部多个地区的快速出现和地理传播。系统发育分析表征了两个季节的 HA 分支组成,并将希腊菌株定位在欧洲和全球背景下。来自希腊南部的 K 亚支菌株的序列在系统发育上与更新的 2026-2027 疫苗 菌株一致,与 ECDC 和 WHO 监测 数据一致。Whole-基因组 分析证实,在具有完全组装的聚合酶片段的所有分离株中不存在毒力相关聚合酶标记,并鉴定了与循环亚进化枝共分离的保守内部片段取代模式。这些发现提供了第一个发布的希腊南部流感的全 基因组 进化数据集,有助于深入了解亚型和分支水平的进化动态,并在国家参考实验室水平证明持续 基因组监测 的公共卫生价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查希腊南部连续两个季节的 季节性流感 病毒的基因组 进化,强调 基因组监测 对于追踪病毒 进化 并为 疫苗 菌株选择提供信息的重要性。
设计与对象
该研究在2024-2025年和2025-2026年流感季节期间在希腊南部进行了系统性的基于全基因组的流感监测,分析了总共40个具有完整或接近完整基因组组装的样本。
核心方法
研究人员使用牛津纳米孔技术 (ONT) 测序来表征连续两个季节(2024-2025 年和 2025-2026 年)在希腊南部传播的流感病毒。
主要结果
该研究确定了 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 从 C.1.9.3 分支到 D.3.1/D.3.1.1 的完整的季节间谱系转变,以及 甲型H3N2流感病毒 K 分支在希腊南部多个地区的快速出现和地理传播。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了 季节性流感 病毒的动态进化模式,并证明了持续的 基因组监测 在理解亚型和进化枝水平变化方面的价值。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果有助于了解希腊南部的流感 进化,并支持 基因组监测 对于 疫苗 菌株选择和公共卫生准备的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量相对较小且关注特定地理区域的限制,但由于使用了全 基因组 测序并与国际 监测 数据进行比对,因此证据充足。

Genomic Surveillance and Evolution of Influenza Virus Across Two Consecutive Seasons, 2024–2026, in Southern Greece

Authors: Konstantina Dragoumani, Georgia Tzouganatou, Maria Evangelidou, Marios Konstantinos Sifakis, Maria Tsintziloni, Emmanouil Angelakis, Mary Emmanouil
Original abstract (English)
Continuous genomic surveillance of influenza virus is critical for monitoring viral evolution, detecting emerging variants, and informing vaccine strain selection. While partial-gene Sanger sequencing has traditionally underpinned national surveillance programs, whole-genome sequencing (WGS) enables simultaneous characterization of all eight genomic segments, providing resolution not achievable through conventional approaches. This is the first published, systematic, whole-genome-based influenza surveillance study reported from Greece. We sequenced and characterized influenza viruses circulating in Southern Greece (S. Greece) during two consecutive influenza seasons, 2024–2025 and 2025–2026, using Oxford Nanopore Technologies (ONT) sequencing. A total of 40 samples were analyzed, with complete or near-complete genome assembly achieved in the vast majority of them. During 2024–2025, co-circulation of A(H1N1)pdm09 (subclades C.1.9.3 and D.3.1), A(H3N2) (subclades J.1.1 and J.2.2), and B/Victoria (C.5 subclades, predominantly C.5.7) influenza viruses was confirmed, with the detection of an oseltamivir-resistant H1N1 variant carrying NA H275Y as a clinically significant finding. Extension of surveillance into 2025–2026 revealed a complete inter-seasonal lineage transition in A(H1N1)pdm09 from subclade C.1.9.3 to D.3.1/D.3.1.1, and the rapid emergence and geographic dissemination of A(H3N2) subclade K across multiple regions of Southern Greece. Phylogenetic analysis characterized the HA subclade composition across the two seasons and positioned the Greek strains within the European and global context. Sequences of subclade K strains from S. Greece were phylogenetically concordant with the updated 2026–2027 vaccine strains, in line with ECDC and WHO surveillance data. Whole-genome analysis confirmed the absence of virulence-associated polymerase markers in all isolates with fully assembled polymerase segments, and identified conserved internal segment substitution patterns co-segregating with circulating subclades. These findings provide the first published whole-genome evolutionary dataset for influenza in Southern Greece, contributing insight into subtype and clade-level evolutionary dynamics and demonstrating the public health value of sustained genomic surveillance at a national reference laboratory level.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the genomic evolution of seasonal influenza viruses across two consecutive seasons in Southern Greece, emphasizing the importance of genomic surveillance for tracking viral evolution and informing vaccine strain selection.
Design and population
The study conducted a systematic whole-genome-based influenza surveillance in Southern Greece during the 2024–2025 and 2025–2026 influenza seasons, analyzing a total of 40 samples with complete or near-complete genome assembly.
Core methods
The researchers used Oxford Nanopore Technologies (ONT) sequencing to characterize influenza viruses circulating in Southern Greece during the two consecutive seasons, 2024–2025 and 2025–2026.
Main results
The study identified a complete inter-seasonal lineage transition in A(H1N1)pdm09 from subclade C.1.9.3 to D.3.1/D.3.1.1 and the rapid emergence and geographic dissemination of A(H3N2) subclade K across multiple regions of Southern Greece.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the dynamic evolutionary patterns of seasonal influenza viruses and demonstrate the value of sustained genomic surveillance in understanding subtype and clade-level changes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results contribute to the understanding of influenza evolution in Southern Greece and support the importance of genomic surveillance for vaccine strain selection and public health preparedness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its relatively small sample size and focus on a specific geographic region, but the evidence is strong due to the use of whole-genome sequencing and alignment with international surveillance data.

2019-2024 年住院成人甲型流感的基因组和临床分析:来自单中心的神经氨酸酶和聚合酶酸性蛋白 突变 结果关联的探索性证据。

Genomic and Clinical Analysis of Influenza A in Hospitalized Adults, 2019-2024: Exploratory Evidence on Neuraminidase and Polymerase Acidic Protein Mutation-Outcome Associations From a Single Center.
作者： Feng A, Tan W, Xu W, Zhang X, Wei D, Yang Z
摘要中文翻译背景 抗病毒药物 被广泛用于治疗流感,但潜在耐药性 突变 仍然是一个重大挑战,并且关键耐药性 突变 的临床数据有限。方法 我们对 2019 年至 2024 年间上海瑞金医院收治的 765 名经 RT-PCR 确诊的甲型流感成人患者进行了回顾性 队列研究 研究,以确定在与关键 宿主 因素一起分析时,特定的 NA/PA 队列研究 是否与严重结局或住院时间延长 (LOS) 相关。获得 PA 和 NA 序列并将其与临床 队列研究 关联以进行 突变 临床结果分析。通过使用 Benjamini-Hochberg 程序控制错误发现率来解决多重测试问题。结果 765例甲型流感病毒感染住院患者中,中位年龄为68岁;5.0%为重症,9.9%死亡。年龄较大、较高的 CURB-65 评分和细菌/真菌合并感染是 死亡率 的主要相关因素,而 抗病毒药物 方案和 Ct 值与 死亡率 或严重结局不独立相关。调整后的观察分析显示,巴洛沙韦对严重疾病的估计较低,但与院内死亡无关。主要高层阻力位突变不存在;检测到的 NA/PA 突变 与严重疾病或 死亡率 没有显着相关性。PA-F105L 显示出与较长 LOS 的探索性关联,但这一发现在调整后减弱,并且受到进化枝和日历周期的混淆。结论 临床结果主要由 宿主 因素和入院严重程度决定。检测到的 NA/PA 突变 与严重后果没有显着相关性,同时无法评估高水平耐药性;PA-F105L-LOS 关联需要表型和外部验证。

问题与背景

设计与对象

核心方法
获得 PA 和 NA 序列并将其与临床 队列研究 关联以进行 突变 临床结果分析。

解释与创新
年龄较大、较高的 CURB-65 评分和细菌/真菌合并感染是 死亡率 的主要相关因素,而 抗病毒药物 方案和 Ct 值与 死亡率 或严重结局不独立相关。检测到的 NA/PA 突变 与严重后果没有显着相关性,同时无法评估高水平耐药性;PA-F105L-LOS 关联需要表型和外部验证。
科研与公卫意义
临床结果主要由 宿主 因素和入院严重程度决定。
局限与证据强度
通过使用 Benjamini-Hochberg 程序控制错误发现率来解决多重测试问题。提供的证据没有报告与研究设计或人群相关的任何具体限制。

Genomic and Clinical Analysis of Influenza A in Hospitalized Adults, 2019-2024: Exploratory Evidence on Neuraminidase and Polymerase Acidic Protein Mutation-Outcome Associations From a Single Center.

Authors: Feng A, Tan W, Xu W, Zhang X, Wei D, Yang Z
Original abstract (English)
Background Antivirals are widely used to treat influenza, but potential resistance mutations remain a major challenge, and clinical data on key resistance mutations are limited. Methods We conducted a retrospective cohort study of 765 adults with RT-PCR-confirmed influenza A admitted to Ruijin Hospital, Shanghai, between 2019 and 2024 to determine whether specific NA/PA mutations are associated with severe outcomes or prolonged length of stay (LOS) when analyzed alongside key host factors. PA and NA sequences were obtained and linked to the clinical cohort for mutation-clinical outcome analyses. Multiple testing was addressed by controlling the false discovery rate using the Benjamini-Hochberg procedure. Results Among 765 inpatients with influenza A virus infection, the median age was 68 years; 5.0% were severe disease and 9.9% died. Older age, higher CURB-65 score, and bacterial/fungal co-infection were the main correlates of mortality, whereas antiviral regimen and Ct value were not independently associated with mortality or severe outcomes. Adjusted observational analyses showed a lower estimate for severe disease with baloxavir but no association with in-hospital death. Major high-level resistance mutations were absent; detected NA/PA mutations showed no robust association with severe disease or mortality. PA-F105L showed an exploratory association with longer LOS, but this finding was attenuated after adjustment and was confounded by clade and calendar period. Conclusions Clinical outcomes were driven mainly by host factors and admission severity. Detected NA/PA mutations were not robustly associated with severe outcomes, while high-level resistance could not be assessed; PA-F105L-LOS association requires phenotypic and external validation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Antivirals are widely used to treat influenza, but potential resistance mutations remain a major challenge, and clinical data on key resistance mutations are limited.
Design and population
We conducted a retrospective cohort study of 765 adults with RT-PCR-confirmed influenza A admitted to Ruijin Hospital, Shanghai, between 2019 and 2024 to determine whether specific NA/PA mutations are associated with severe outcomes or prolonged length of stay (LOS) when analyzed alongside key host factors.
Core methods
PA and NA sequences were obtained and linked to the clinical cohort for mutation-clinical outcome analyses.
Main results
Among 765 inpatients with influenza A virus infection, the median age was 68 years; 5.0% were severe disease and 9.9% died. Major high-level resistance mutations were absent; detected NA/PA mutations showed no robust association with severe disease or mortality. PA-F105L showed an exploratory association with longer LOS, but this finding was attenuated after adjustment and was confounded by clade and calendar period.
Interpretation and novelty
Older age, higher CURB-65 score, and bacterial/fungal co-infection were the main correlates of mortality, whereas antiviral regimen and Ct value were not independently associated with mortality or severe outcomes. Detected NA/PA mutations were not robustly associated with severe outcomes, while high-level resistance could not be assessed; PA-F105L-LOS association requires phenotypic and external validation.
Scientific and public-health significance
Clinical outcomes were driven mainly by host factors and admission severity.
Limitations and evidence strength
Multiple testing was addressed by controlling the false discovery rate using the Benjamini-Hochberg procedure. The supplied evidence does not report any specific limitations related to the study design or population.

地球上丰富的高岭土衍生 抗病毒药物 涂层通过三重协同捕获-失活-阻断机制。

Earth-Abundant Kaolin Derived Antiviral Coating via Triple Synergistic Capture-Inactivate-Block Mechanism.
作者： Jiangpeng Ding, Donghui Sun, Hao Wang, Huaming Yang
摘要中文翻译抗病毒药物 涂层转化受到添加剂聚集和树脂不相容性的限制。我们通过热活化和与铜前体的一锅球磨将天然高岭土升级为高效的 抗病毒药物 添加剂,产生均匀装饰有铜颗粒 (Cu Ps) 的无定形偏高岭土。这种无溶剂方案可抑制 Cu Ps 团聚,并确保有机硅丙烯酸粘合剂中的均匀分散,这对于涂层的完整性至关重要。偏高岭土支架充当高亲和力的病毒捕获平台,而锚定的Cu Ps协同产生超氧自由基(•O2-)并维持局部Cu+/Cu2+释放,从而实现双模式氧化失活。与树脂的物理屏障相结合,这种捕获-氧化-阻断机制在环境条件下可实现 >99.9% 的甲型流感 H3N2 和肠道病毒 EV-71 灭活。该复合材料支持公斤级生产(批量 2.22 公斤),在超过 6000 次洗涤循环后仍保持完整活性,并通过皮肤安全测试。这项工作将低成本粘土矿物转化为结构工程 抗病毒药物 材料,并提供了一种可扩展的路线,克服了表面保护方面持续的可制造性和耐久性限制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过开发一种新型高岭土 抗病毒药物 添加剂来解决 抗病毒药物 涂料技术的局限性,特别是添加剂聚集和树脂不相容性。
设计与对象
该研究涉及通过热活化和与铜前体的一锅球磨将天然高岭土升级为高效的 抗病毒药物 添加剂,从而产生均匀装饰有铜颗粒 (Cu Ps) 的无定形偏高岭土。
核心方法
采用无溶剂方案来抑制 Cu Ps 团聚并确保有机硅丙烯酸粘合剂中的均匀分散,这对于涂层的完整性至关重要。
主要结果
偏高岭土支架充当高亲和力的病毒捕获平台,而锚定的Cu Ps协同产生超氧自由基(•O2-)并持续局部Cu+/Cu2+释放,从而实现双模式氧化失活。该复合材料在环境条件下对甲型流感 H3N2 和肠道病毒 EV-71 的灭活率大于 99.9%。
解释与创新
这项工作将低成本粘土矿物转化为结构工程 抗病毒药物 材料,提供了一种可扩展的路线,克服了表面保护方面持续的可制造性和耐久性限制。
科研与公卫意义
该复合材料支持公斤级生产,在超过 6000 次洗涤循环后仍能保持完整活性,并通过皮肤安全测试,为 抗病毒药物 涂层技术的广泛应用提供了潜力。
局限与证据强度
这项工作没有报告任何具体的设计限制或证据强度评估。

Earth-Abundant Kaolin Derived Antiviral Coating via Triple Synergistic Capture-Inactivate-Block Mechanism.

Authors: Jiangpeng Ding, Donghui Sun, Hao Wang, Huaming Yang
Original abstract (English)
Antiviral coating translation is constrained by additive aggregation and resin incompatibility. We upgrade natural kaolin into an efficient antiviral additive via thermal activation and one-pot ball milling with copper precursors, yielding amorphous metakaolin uniformly decorated with copper particles (Cu Ps). This solvent-free protocol suppresses Cu Ps agglomeration and ensures homogeneous dispersion within silicone-acrylic binders, essential for coating integrity. The metakaolin scaffold serves as a high-affinity viral capture platform, while anchored Cu Ps synergistically generate superoxide radicals (•O2-) and sustain localized Cu+/Cu2+ release, enabling dual-modal oxidative inactivation. Combined with the resin's physical barrier, this capture-oxidize-block mechanism delivers >99.9% inactivation of influenza A H3N2 and enterovirus EV-71 under ambient conditions. The composite supports kilogram-scale production (2.22 kg batch), maintains full activity after >6000 washing cycles, and passes dermal safety tests. This work transforms a low-cost clay mineral into a structurally engineered antiviral material and provides a scalable route that overcomes persistent manufacturability and durability limitations in surface protection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limitations in antiviral coating technology, particularly additive aggregation and resin incompatibility, by developing a novel antiviral additive from kaolin.
Design and population
The research involved upgrading natural kaolin into an efficient antiviral additive via thermal activation and one-pot ball milling with copper precursors, resulting in amorphous metakaolin uniformly decorated with copper particles (Cu Ps).
Core methods
A solvent-free protocol was used to suppress Cu Ps agglomeration and ensure homogeneous dispersion within silicone-acrylic binders, essential for coating integrity.
Main results
The metakaolin scaffold served as a high-affinity viral capture platform, while anchored Cu Ps synergistically generated superoxide radicals (•O2-) and sustained localized Cu+/Cu2+ release, enabling dual-modal oxidative inactivation. The composite delivered >99.9% inactivation of influenza A H3N2 and enterovirus EV-71 under ambient conditions.
Interpretation and novelty
The work transformed a low-cost clay mineral into a structurally engineered antiviral material, providing a scalable route that overcomes persistent manufacturability and durability limitations in surface protection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The composite supports kilogram-scale production, maintains full activity after >6000 washing cycles, and passes dermal safety tests, offering potential for widespread application in antiviral coating technology.
Limitations and evidence strength
This work does not report any specific design limitations or evidence strength assessments.

加拿大安大略省与呼吸道病毒相关的人群归因 死亡率:基于人群的生态分析

Population attributable mortality associated with respiratory viruses in Ontario, Canada: a population-based ecological analysis
作者： David N. Fisman, Alicia A. Grima, Natalie Wilson, Simran Mann, Ashleigh R. Tuite, Eunyoung Lee
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 呼吸道病毒是 死亡率 群体的主要贡献者,但死因编码低估了其影响。将病毒循环与 死亡率 波动联系起来的生态回归模型可以解决这一限制。目的 估计加拿大安大略省(1993-2025 年)与甲型和乙型流感、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和 SARS-CoV-2 相关的 死亡率 的人群归因分数 (PAF),并描述大流行过渡期间病毒归因 死亡率 的时间变化。方法 我们利用实验室 监测 指标分析了甲型、乙型、RSV 和 SARS-CoV-2 流感的每月全因 死亡率 数据。具有长期趋势、傅里叶季节性项和人口偏移的负二项式模型适合大流行前(1993年1月至2020年2月)和大流行合并时期(2020年3月至2025年2月)。PAF 源自将病毒系数设置为零的反事实预测。敏感性分析检查了大流行期间的时间分层(国际关注的突发公共卫生事件 [PHEIC] 期间:2020 年 3 月至 2023 年 4 月;PHEIC 后:2023 年 5 月至 2025 年 2 月),排除了大流行早期(2020 年 3 月至 6 月),以及没有傅里叶季节调整的模型。Wald 测试比较了不同规格的系数。结果 大流行前,甲型流感占 死亡率 的 1.8%(95% CI 1.4–2.3%);乙型流感没有显示出可检测到的影响。RSV 在经季节性调整的模型中表现出负相关,但在未经季节调整的情况下呈正相关(PAF 1.9%,95% CI 1.3–2.4%)。在整个大流行期间(2020年3月至2025年2月;n=60个月),在基线死亡率升高(IRR 1.050,P=0.027)的情况下,SARS-CoV-2占死亡人数的6.1%(95% CI 4.2-8.0%),大约是大流行前甲型流感负担的4倍,尽管疫苗接种和抗病毒药物 可用性。模型估计的 SARS-CoV-2 死亡人数与同期安大略省公共卫生部门报告的 COVID-19 死亡人数非常吻合。时间分层发现,PHEIC 后时期 SARS-CoV-2 归因的 死亡率 显着增加(PAF 9.8%,95% CI 1.1-17.7%;p=0.027),而 PHEIC 后甲型和 B 型流感归因分数与大流行前的基线没有显着差异。排除 2020 年 3 月至 6 月,保守的 SARS-CoV-2 PAF 为 5.7%(95% CI 3.3-8.1%),证实了初步估计的稳健性。荟萃分析显示,不同建模方法中甲型流感 (I2=92.8%) 和 RSV (I2=89.1%) 存在显着异质性,但 SARS-CoV-2 异质性极小 (I2=5.5%)。结论 尽管有可用的对策,但 SARS-CoV-2 与 死亡率 人群负担比 季节性流感 A 高 3-4 倍相关。PHEIC 后的数据表明,呼吸道病毒 死亡率(包括流感病毒)的负担尚未恢复到大流行前的水平,这凸显了呼吸道病毒预防策略的持续重要性。甲型流感和 RSV 的估计对季节性调整敏感,突出了在量化病毒归因 死亡率 时建模选择的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道病毒是 死亡率 群体的主要贡献者,但死因编码低估了其影响。该研究旨在估计与特定呼吸道病毒相关的 死亡率 的人群归因分数。
设计与对象
使用 1993 年 1 月至 2025 年 2 月加拿大安大略省每月全因 死亡率 数据进行了生态分析。该研究重点关注甲型和乙型流感、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和 SARS-CoV-2。
核心方法
使用具有长期趋势、傅里叶季节项和人口偏移的负二项式模型来分析 死亡率 数据。敏感性分析检查了不同的时间分层和建模方法。
主要结果
大流行前,甲型流感占 死亡率 的 1.8%(95% CI 1.4-2.3%),而乙型流感没有显示出可检测到的影响。在整个大流行期间,SARS-CoV-2 占死亡人数的 6.1%(95% CI 4.2-8.0%),比大流行前的甲型流感负担高出约 4 倍。
解释与创新
研究发现,尽管有对策,但 SARS-CoV-2 与 死亡率 人群负担显着高于 季节性流感 A 相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了呼吸道病毒预防策略的持续重要性,因为公共卫生事件后的数据表明,呼吸道病毒的 死亡率 负担尚未恢复到大流行前的水平。
局限与证据强度
该研究对甲型流感和 RSV 的估计对季节性调整很敏感,表明基于模型选择的结果存在潜在的变异性。由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

Population attributable mortality associated with respiratory viruses in Ontario, Canada: a population-based ecological analysis

Authors: David N. Fisman, Alicia A. Grima, Natalie Wilson, Simran Mann, Ashleigh R. Tuite, Eunyoung Lee
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Respiratory viruses are major contributors to population mortality, but cause-of-death coding undercounts their impact. Ecological regression models linking viral circulation to mortality fluctuations can address this limitation. Aim To estimate the population attributable fraction (PAF) of mortality associated with influenza A and B, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), and SARS-CoV-2 in Ontario, Canada (1993–2025), and to characterize temporal changes in virus-attributable mortality across the pandemic transition. Methods We analysed monthly all-cause mortality data with laboratory surveillance indicators for influenza A, B, RSV, and SARS-CoV-2. Negative binomial models with secular trends, Fourier seasonal terms, and population offsets were fit for pre-pandemic (January 1993–February 2020) and combined pandemic (March 2020–February 2025) periods. PAFs were derived from counterfactual predictions setting viral coefficients to zero. Sensitivity analyses examined temporal stratification of the pandemic period (Public Health Emergency of International Concern [PHEIC] period: March 2020–April 2023; post-PHEIC: May 2023–February 2025), exclusion of the early pandemic period (March–June 2020), and models without Fourier seasonal adjustment. Wald tests compared coefficients across specifications. Results Pre-pandemic, influenza A accounted for 1.8% (95% CI 1.4–2.3%) of mortality; influenza B showed no detectable impact. RSV demonstrated inverse associations in seasonally adjusted models but positive associations (PAF 1.9%, 95% CI 1.3–2.4%) without seasonal adjustment. Over the combined pandemic period (March 2020–February 2025; n=60 months), amid elevated baseline mortality (IRR 1.050, P=0.027), SARS-CoV-2 accounted for 6.1% (95% CI 4.2–8.0%) of deaths, approximately 4-fold the pre-pandemic influenza A burden, despite widespread vaccination and antiviral availability. Model-estimated SARS-CoV-2-attributable deaths closely matched reported COVID-19 deaths from Public Health Ontario over the same period. Temporal stratification identified a significant increase in SARS-CoV-2-attributable mortality in the post-PHEIC period (PAF 9.8%, 95% CI 1.1–17.7%; p=0.027), while post-PHEIC influenza A and B attributable fractions did not differ significantly from pre-pandemic baselines. Excluding March-June 2020 yielded a conservative SARS-CoV-2 PAF of 5.7% (95% CI 3.3–8.1%), confirming robustness of primary estimates. Meta-analyses showed substantial heterogeneity for influenza A (I2=92.8%) and RSV (I2=89.1%) across modeling approaches, but minimal heterogeneity for SARS-CoV-2 (I2=5.5%). Conclusion SARS-CoV-2 was associated with a 3–4-fold higher population mortality burden than seasonal influenza A despite available countermeasures. Post-PHEIC data suggest that the burden of respiratory virus mortality, including for influenza, has not returned to pre-pandemic levels, highlighting the continued importance of respiratory virus prevention strategies. Estimates for influenza A and RSV were sensitive to seasonal adjustment, highlighting the importance of modelling choices when quantifying virus-attributable mortality.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory viruses are major contributors to population mortality, but cause-of-death coding undercounts their impact. The study aimed to estimate the population attributable fraction of mortality associated with specific respiratory viruses.
Design and population
An ecological analysis was conducted using monthly all-cause mortality data in Ontario, Canada, from January 1993 to February 2025. The study focused on influenza A and B, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), and SARS-CoV-2.
Core methods
Negative binomial models with secular trends, Fourier seasonal terms, and population offsets were used to analyze mortality data. Sensitivity analyses examined different temporal stratifications and modeling approaches.
Main results
Pre-pandemic, influenza A accounted for 1.8% (95% CI 1.4–2.3%) of mortality, while influenza B showed no detectable impact. Over the combined pandemic period, SARS-CoV-2 accounted for 6.1% (95% CI 4.2–8.0%) of deaths, which was approximately 4-fold higher than pre-pandemic influenza A burden.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that SARS-CoV-2 was associated with a significantly higher population mortality burden than seasonal influenza A, despite the availability of countermeasures.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the continued importance of respiratory virus prevention strategies, as post-PHEIC data suggest that the mortality burden from respiratory viruses has not returned to pre-pandemic levels.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's estimates for influenza A and RSV were sensitive to seasonal adjustment, indicating potential variability in results based on modeling choices. The evidence strength is moderate due to these limitations.

赞比亚农村地区设施呼吸道病原体 监测 的空气采样:与人类 监测 的前瞻性比较

Air sampling for facility-based respiratory pathogen surveillance in rural Zambia: a prospective comparison with human surveillance
作者： Samuel L. Baron, Mutinta Hamahuwa, Passwell Munachoonga, Pamela Sinywimaanzi, Thomas Aichele, Katherine Fenstermacher, Richard Eric Rothman, Andrew S Pekosz, Edgar Simulundu, Shelby L. O’Connor
摘要中文翻译呼吸道感染是全球急性疾病的主要原因,给能力有限的低收入和中等收入国家带来不成比例的负担。虽然基于设施的 监测 仍然是人群呼吸道病原体监测的标准,但环境空气采样作为一种非侵入性补充方法具有潜在的优势。2025 年 7 月至 2026 年 6 月,我们在赞比亚南部省马查医院针对正在发生的呼吸道疾病 监测 进行了一项前瞻性环境空气采样研究。在门诊部和三个住院病房连续部署空气采样器,每周收集和测试 3 次过滤器。监测 全年在患者人群中进行,采集自流感样疾病患者的鼻咽样本。使用 Cepheid GeneXpert 平台对所有样本进行了甲型/乙型流感病毒、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和 SARS-CoV-2 检测。我们使用一致性百分比、Cohen's Kappa、Spearman 相关性以及带有广义估计方程的逻辑回归来分析空气和鼻咽样本之间的一致性。12 个月内,分析了 596 个空气样本和 701 个人体样本。甲型流感病毒是最常见的病原体,在 38 个空气样本和 87 个鼻咽样本中检测到,大多数病例是在 2025 年 7 月至 9 月期间检测到的。对于甲型流感,空气样本和鼻咽样本之间存在很强的同周一致性(总体一致性为 80.4%;阳性一致性为 44.4%;Cohen's Kappa = 0.5;Spearman's rho = 0.57)。SARS-CoV-2 也很常见,在 18 个空气样本和 16 个鼻咽样本中检测到,全年都有零星病例发现。对于 SARS-CoV-2,空气样本和鼻咽样本之间的一致性有限(68.6% 一致;23.8% 阳性一致;Cohen's Kappa = 0.2;Spearman's rho = 0.2)。B 型流感病毒(1 个空气样本和 13 个鼻咽样本呈阳性)和 RSV(0 个空气样本和 4 个鼻咽样本呈阳性)零星传播,并且毫不奇怪,样本之间的一致性有限。阳性空气样本的甲型流感病毒循环阈值(目标 1 的中位数:36.7 vs. 25.1;p<0.001;目标 2 的中位数:39.5 vs. 28.6;p<0.001)和 SARS-CoV-2(中位数:41.3 vs. 27.5;p<0.001)显着高于鼻咽样本。记录了实施挑战,包括间歇性断电和电源插座损坏导致采样器关闭,以及需要对设施工作人员和患者进行宣传活动的研究意识问题。空气采样是可行的,并且被证明有望作为农村资源有限环境中基于设施的呼吸道病原体 监测 的补充方法。这些发现支持将环境空气采样与 监测 结合到患者群体中,作为呼吸道病原体监测多模式方法的一部分。

问题与背景
呼吸道感染是全球急性疾病的主要原因,给能力有限的低收入和中等收入国家带来不成比例的负担。虽然基于设施的 监测 仍然是人群呼吸道病原体监测的标准,但环境空气采样作为一种非侵入性补充方法具有潜在的优势。

解释与创新
研究表明,空气采样可以与人类 监测 并行检测呼吸道病原体,根据病毒的不同,具有不同程度的一致性。这些发现支持将环境空气采样与 监测 结合到患者群体中,作为呼吸道病原体监测多模式方法的一部分。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,空气采样可能是农村资源有限环境中呼吸道病原体 监测 的一个有价值的工具,补充了传统的基于人类的方法。这可以提高 监测 系统在医疗基础设施有限的地区的及时性和覆盖范围。
局限与证据强度
记录了实施挑战,包括间歇性断电和电源插座损坏导致采样器关闭,以及需要对设施工作人员和患者进行宣传活动的研究意识问题。由于这些后勤限制和潜在的抽样偏差,证据是中等的。

Air sampling for facility-based respiratory pathogen surveillance in rural Zambia: a prospective comparison with human surveillance

Authors: Samuel L. Baron, Mutinta Hamahuwa, Passwell Munachoonga, Pamela Sinywimaanzi, Thomas Aichele, Katherine Fenstermacher, Richard Eric Rothman, Andrew S Pekosz, Edgar Simulundu, Shelby L. O’Connor
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory infections are the leading cause of acute disease incidence globally and disproportionately burden low- and middle-income countries where surveillance capacity is limited. While facility-based surveillance remains the standard for respiratory pathogen monitoring in human populations, environmental air sampling offers potential advantages as a non-invasive supplementary method. We conducted a prospective environmental air sampling study nested within ongoing respiratory disease surveillance at Macha Hospital in Southern Province, Zambia, from July 2025 to June 2026. Air samplers were deployed continuously in the outpatient department and three inpatient wards, with filters collected and tested three times weekly. Surveillance in the patient population was conducted year-round with nasopharyngeal samples collected from patients with influenza-like illness. All samples were tested for influenza A/B virus, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), and SARS-CoV-2 using the Cepheid GeneXpert platform. We analyzed concordance between air and nasopharyngeal samples using percent agreement, Cohen's Kappa, Spearman correlations, and logistic regression with generalized estimating equations. Over 12 months, 596 air samples and 701 human samples were analyzed. Influenza A virus was the most common pathogen, detected in 38 air samples and 87 nasopharyngeal samples, with most cases detected from July to September 2025. For Influenza A, there was strong same-week concordance between air and nasopharyngeal samples (80.4% overall agreement; 44.4% positive agreement; Cohen's Kappa = 0.5; Spearman's rho = 0.57). SARS-CoV-2 was also common, detected in 18 air samples and 16 nasopharyngeal samples, with cases detected sporadically throughout the year. For SARS-CoV-2, there was limited concordance between air and nasopharyngeal samples (68.6% agreement; 23.8% positive agreement; Cohen's Kappa = 0.2; Spearman's rho = 0.2). Influenza B virus (1 air and 13 nasopharyngeal samples positive) and RSV (0 air and 4 nasopharyngeal samples positive) circulated sporadically, and unsurprisingly, gave limited concordance between samples. Cycle threshold values for influenza A virus (median for target 1: 36.7 vs. 25.1; p<0.001; median for target 2: 39.5 vs. 28.6; p<0.001) and SARS-CoV-2 (median: 41.3 vs. 27.5; p<0.001) were significantly higher for positive air samples than for nasopharyngeal samples. Implementation challenges were documented, including intermittent power outages and compromised electrical outlets causing sampler shutdowns, as well as study awareness issues that required sensitization activities for both facility staff and patients. Air sampling was feasible and demonstrated promise as a supplementary method for facility-based respiratory pathogen surveillance in rural resource-limited settings. These findings support integrating environmental air sampling alongside surveillance in patient populations as part of a multimodal approach to respiratory pathogen monitoring.

While facility-based surveillance remains the standard for respiratory pathogen monitoring in human populations, environmental air sampling offers potential advantages as a non-invasive supplementary method.

Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrates that air sampling can detect respiratory pathogens in parallel with human surveillance, with varying degrees of concordance depending on the virus. These findings support integrating environmental air sampling alongside surveillance in patient populations as part of a multimodal approach to respiratory pathogen monitoring.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that air sampling could be a valuable tool for respiratory pathogen surveillance in rural resource-limited settings, complementing traditional human-based methods. This could improve the timeliness and coverage of surveillance systems in areas with limited healthcare infrastructure.
Limitations and evidence strength
Implementation challenges were documented, including intermittent power outages and compromised electrical outlets causing sampler shutdowns, as well as study awareness issues that required sensitization activities for both facility staff and patients. The evidence is moderate due to these logistical limitations and the potential for sampling bias.

口腔健康状况与 COVID-19 和流感感染之间的关系

The relationship between the oral health status and COVID-19 and Influenza infections
作者： Kosuke Tonouchi, Hironori Sakai, Ryo Kajihara, Sato Shimizu, Hironobu Fukuda, Hiroki Otagiri, Kiriko Matsuzawa, Hiroshi Kurita
摘要中文翻译摘要 据报道,口腔卫生与流感感染之间存在关系。2019 年冠状病毒病 (COVID-19) 和流感是主要的呼吸道病毒感染。口腔可能充当这些病毒的进入位点和 储存宿主。本研究通过 流行病学 和组织病理学研究了口腔健康在 SARS-CoV-2 感染中的作用。还进行了一项前瞻性观察研究,以调查良好的口腔卫生习惯是否有助于预防呼吸道病毒感染。在使用健康/牙科检查和日本国民健康保险数据库数据的流行病学研究中,多变量分析确定口腔卫生不良是 COVID-19 发病的独立危险因素。组织病理学分析显示,有炎症的牙龈上皮中血管紧张素转换酶 2 (ACE2) 受体和弗林蛋白酶的表达高于没有炎症的牙龈上皮。这项前瞻性观察性研究比较了两组具有不同口腔卫生习惯的人群,结果表明,使用牙间刷和漱口水等良好的口腔卫生习惯可以预防 COVID-19 和流感的发作。总的来说,这些结果表明,不良的口腔卫生会增加 ACE2 和弗林蛋白酶的表达,从而导致 COVID-19 感染的发生。此外,改善口腔卫生可以预防 COVID-19 和流感的发作。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查口腔健康状况与呼吸道病毒感染(包括流感)发病之间的关系。
设计与对象
进行了一项前瞻性观察研究,比较具有不同口腔卫生习惯的个体,重点关注他们发生呼吸道病毒感染的风险。
核心方法
该研究使用了健康和牙科检查的流行病学数据以及日本国民健康保险数据库,以及牙龈上皮的组织病理学分析。
主要结果
多变量分析确定口腔卫生不良是 COVID-19 发病的独立危险因素,组织病理学分析显示发炎的牙龈上皮中 ACE2 受体和弗林蛋白酶表达较高。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,不良的口腔卫生可能会通过改变受体表达来增加对呼吸道病毒感染的易感性,这为口腔健康在病毒感染中的作用提供了新的视角。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了保持口腔卫生作为预防呼吸道病毒感染(包括流感)的潜在预防措施的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究是观察性的,依赖于自我报告的数据,这可能会引入偏差。然而,这些证据得到了流行病学和组织病理学分析的支持,提供了中等程度的置信度。

The relationship between the oral health status and COVID-19 and Influenza infections

Authors: Kosuke Tonouchi, Hironori Sakai, Ryo Kajihara, Sato Shimizu, Hironobu Fukuda, Hiroki Otagiri, Kiriko Matsuzawa, Hiroshi Kurita
Original abstract (English)
Abstract A relationship has been reported between oral hygiene and influenza infection. Coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) and influenza are major respiratory virus infections. The oral cavity may act as both an entry site and reservoir for these viruses. The present study examined the role of oral health in SARS-CoV-2 infection through epidemiology and histopathology. A prospective observational study was also conducted to investigate whether good oral hygiene habits contribute to the prevention of respiratory viral infections. In the epidemiological study using data from health/dental check-ups and the Japanese National Health Insurance database, a multivariate analysis identified poor oral hygiene as an independent risk factor for the onset of COVID-19. A histopathological analysis revealed that the expression of the angiotensin-converting enzyme 2 (ACE2) receptor and furin in the gingival epithelium was higher with than without inflammation. The prospective observational study comparing two groups with different oral hygiene habits suggested that good oral hygiene practices with interdental brushes and mouthwash prevented the onset of COVID-19 and influenza. Collectively, these results indicate that poor oral hygiene contributes to the onset of COVID-19 infection by increasing ACE2 and furin expression. Furthermore, improvements in oral hygiene may prevent the onset of COVID-19 and influenza.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the relationship between oral health status and the onset of respiratory viral infections, including influenza.
Design and population
A prospective observational study was conducted to compare individuals with different oral hygiene habits, focusing on their risk of developing respiratory viral infections.
Core methods
The study used epidemiological data from health and dental check-ups and the Japanese National Health Insurance database, along with histopathological analysis of gingival epithelium.
Main results
Multivariate analysis identified poor oral hygiene as an independent risk factor for the onset of COVID-19, and histopathological analysis showed higher ACE2 receptor and furin expression in inflamed gingival epithelium.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that poor oral hygiene may increase susceptibility to respiratory viral infections by altering receptor expression, offering a novel perspective on the role of oral health in viral infections.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the importance of maintaining oral hygiene as a potential preventive measure against respiratory viral infections, including influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is observational and relies on self-reported data, which may introduce bias. However, the evidence is supported by both epidemiological and histopathological analyses, providing a moderate level of confidence.

与未感染 HIV 的成人相比,三价灭活流感 疫苗 对 HIV 感染者的免疫原性和安全性:来自中国的一项研究

Immunogenicity and safety of trivalent inactivated influenza vaccine in adults living with HIV compared with HIV-uninfected adults: a study from China
作者： Su J, Gao K, Han S, Tang X, Guo X, Liang H, Liu X, Xu X, Yan J, Qiu Y
摘要中文翻译摘要目的评价三价灭活流感疫苗(IIV3)在≥18岁HIV感染者中的免疫原性和安全性,并为中国该人群中流感疫苗接种的支持提供证据。方法这项单中心、开放标签的比较分析源自一项更大规模的三组临床研究。在 疫苗接种 后 29 天至 6 个月期间,接种疫苗和未接种疫苗的 HIV 感染者中几乎没有报告流感样疾病事件;这些发现是描述性的,并不用于评估 疫苗 对流感的功效。结论 感染 HIV 的成人在 IIV3 疫苗接种 后产生了显着的抗体反应,尽管反应的程度因流感毒株而异。IIV3 安全且耐受性良好,支持当前针对 HIV 感染者的流感 疫苗接种 的建议。临床试验注册https://www.chictr.org.cn/showprojEN.html?proj=255613,标识符 ChiCTR2500112191。关键词:HIV 感染、免疫原性、安全性、血清保护、三价灭活流感 疫苗 方法研究设计和参与者本研究是一项单中心、开放标签的比较分析,源自在中国浙江省杭州市进行的一项更大规模的三臂临床研究,其中艾滋病毒感染者被随机分配至 疫苗接种 组或无 疫苗接种 组。在本次比较分析中,计划有 472 名接种疫苗的参与者,其中包括 236 名感染艾滋病毒的成年人(第 1 组)和 236 名年龄和性别相当的未感染艾滋病毒的成年人(第 2 组)。两组参与者在第 0 天接受一剂 IIV3 (0.5 mL)。在 疫苗接种 之前和之后 28 天收集血样,用于针对 A 的血凝抑制 (HI) 抗体测试。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估三价灭活流感 疫苗 (IIV3) 在感染 HIV 的成人中与未感染 HIV 的成人相比的免疫原性和安全性,弥补有关 HIV 如何影响该人群中 疫苗 反应的知识差距。
设计与对象
该研究是一项单中心、开放标签的比较分析,源自于中国浙江省杭州市进行的一项更大规模的三组临床研究。其中包括 236 名感染艾滋病毒的成年人(第 1 组)和 236 名年龄和性别相当的未感染艾滋病毒的成年人(第 2 组)。
核心方法
Immunogenicity was assessed using hemagglutination inhibition (HI) antibody testing against A(H1N1),甲型H3N2流感病毒,and B/Victoria strains,with blood samples collected before and 28 days after 疫苗接种.通过在 6 个月的随访期间监测不良事件 (AE) 和严重不良事件 (SAE) 来评估安全性。
主要结果
疫苗接种 后 28 天,A(H1N1) 组 1 和组 2 之间的血清转化率没有显着差异,但 甲型H3N2流感病毒 和 B/Victoria 组 1 的血清转化率显着较低(所有 P < 0.001)。对于所有三种菌株,第 1 组中 疫苗接种 后的 GMT 也显着较低。大多数 AE 均为轻度,未报告 4 级不良反应或 疫苗 相关 SAE。
解释与创新
作者解释说,感染艾滋病毒的成年人在 IIV3 疫苗接种 后产生了显着的抗体反应,尽管反应的程度因流感毒株而异,特别是 甲型H3N2流感病毒。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,IIV3 在成人 HIV 感染者中是安全且耐受性良好的,支持目前针对该人群的流感 疫苗接种 的建议,并强调需要对 HIV 阳性个体中的毒株特异性 免疫应答 进行进一步研究。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括在没有病毒学确认的情况下使用临床症状来识别流感样疾病(ILI),这妨碍了评估 疫苗 的疗效。由于分析的观察性质以及缺乏 ILI 的病毒学确认,证据是中等的。

Immunogenicity and safety of trivalent inactivated influenza vaccine in adults living with HIV compared with HIV-uninfected adults: a study from China

Authors: Su J, Gao K, Han S, Tang X, Guo X, Liang H, Liu X, Xu X, Yan J, Qiu Y
Original abstract (English)
AbstractObjectiveTo evaluate the immunogenicity and safety of trivalent inactivated influenza vaccine (IIV3) in adults aged ≥18 years living with HIV and to provide evidence supporting influenza vaccination in this population in China.MethodsThis single-center, open-label comparative analysis was derived from a larger three-arm clinical study. During the period from 29 days to 6 months after vaccination, few influenza-like illness events were reported among vaccinated and unvaccinated participants living with HIV; these findings were descriptive and were not used to assess vaccine efficacy against influenza.ConclusionAdults living with HIV developed substantial antibody responses following IIV3 vaccination, although the magnitude of response varied across influenza strains. IIV3 was safe and well tolerated, supporting current recommendations for influenza vaccination in adults living with HIV.Clinical trial registrationhttps://www.chictr.org.cn/showprojEN.html?proj=255613, identifier ChiCTR2500112191.Keywords: HIV infection, immunogenicity, safety, seroprotection, trivalent inactivated influenza vaccine MethodsStudy design and participantsThis study was a single-center, open-label comparative analysis derived from a larger three-arm clinical study conducted in Hangzhou, Zhejiang Province, China, in which participants living with HIV were randomized to vaccination or no vaccination. For the present comparative analysis, 472 vaccinated participants were planned, including 236 adults living with HIV (Group 1) and 236 age- and sex-comparable HIV-uninfected adults (Group 2). Participants in both groups received one dose of IIV3 (0.5 mL) on Day 0. Blood samples were collected before and 28 days after vaccination for hemagglutination inhibition (HI) antibody testing against A...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the immunogenicity and safety of trivalent inactivated influenza vaccine (IIV3) in adults living with HIV compared with HIV-uninfected adults, addressing the knowledge gap about how HIV affects vaccine responses in this population.
Design and population
The study was a single-center, open-label comparative analysis derived from a larger three-arm clinical study conducted in Hangzhou, Zhejiang Province, China. It included 236 adults living with HIV (Group 1) and 236 age- and sex-comparable HIV-uninfected adults (Group 2).
Core methods
Immunogenicity was assessed using hemagglutination inhibition (HI) antibody testing against A(H1N1), A(H3N2), and B/Victoria strains, with blood samples collected before and 28 days after vaccination. Safety was evaluated by monitoring adverse events (AEs) and serious adverse events (SAEs) during 6 months of follow-up.
Main results
At 28 days post-vaccination, seroconversion rates did not differ significantly between Group 1 and Group 2 for A(H1N1), but were significantly lower in Group 1 for A(H3N2) and B/Victoria (all P < 0.001). Post-vaccination GMTs were also significantly lower in Group 1 for all three strains. Most AEs were mild, and no Grade 4 adverse reactions or vaccine-related SAEs were reported.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that adults living with HIV developed substantial antibody responses following IIV3 vaccination, although the magnitude of response varied across influenza strains, particularly for A(H3N2).
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that IIV3 is safe and well tolerated in adults living with HIV, supporting current recommendations for influenza vaccination in this population, and highlight the need for further research on strain-specific immune responses in HIV-positive individuals.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the use of clinical symptoms to identify influenza-like illness (ILI) without virological confirmation, which precludes assessing vaccine efficacy. The evidence is moderate due to the observational nature of the analysis and the lack of virological confirmation for ILI.

用社会安全水平补充生物安全水平

Complementing Biosafety Levels With Societal Safety Levels
作者： Kevin M. Esvelt, Dana Gretton, Lennart J. Justen
摘要中文翻译生物安全等级(BSL)分类在降低实验室内的职业风险方面已取得成功,但主要忽视了生物剂可能引发的进一步跨物种溢出(传播)。因此,许多非传染性病原体,其暴露后可能最多导致一个人死亡,需要比1918年流感病毒更高的防控措施。1918年流感病毒曾造成50-100百万人死亡,今天可能引发新的大流行。实验室工作人员是自愿的专业人员,而公众则不是。我们建议在BSL基础上,明确设定预期社会危害的阈值。本文我们基于每年预期的公众死亡人数,正式提出社会安全等级(SSLs)。我们通过(i)实验室释放的概率估计、(ii)社会跨物种溢出的可能性以及(iii)后果来量化这些数值。SSLs将专门适用于具有复制能力的潜在大流行病原体的实验室研究,不会限制对非复制系统或无大流行能力病原体的现场研究、测序或应对措施的开发。我们发现,若将多个重大危害行业普遍采用的每年每设施0.1例死亡的标准应用于潜在大流行病原体的研究,将需要显著的安全改进。在BSL基础上引入SSLs,将为具有大流行潜力的病原体建立可执行的事故安全阈值,使高风险生物研究与有助于其他危险行业获得公众接受的量化安全标准相一致。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
生物制剂的生物安全级别 (BSL) 分类已成功地将实验室内的职业风险降至最低,但在很大程度上忽略了未来 传播 的潜力。该研究解决了对除实验室生物安全之外还考虑社会安全的框架的需求。
设计与对象
该研究是一项概念和政策分析,建议使用社会安全水平(SSL)进行涉及具有复制能力的潜在大流行病原体的实验室研究。它不涉及经验数据收集或特定人群。
核心方法
SSL 是根据每年预期的公众死亡人数正式确定的,计算公式为:(i) 实验室释放的估计概率,(ii) 社会 传播 的可能性,以及 (iii) 后果。这些方法的重点是量化具有大流行能力的病原体的社会危害阈值。
主要结果
我们发现,将多个重大危险行业常见的每设施年预期死亡 0.1 人的标准应用于潜在的大流行病原体研究将需要显着的安全改进。
解释与创新
作者解释说,用 SSL 来补充 BSL 将为能够引起大流行的病原体建立可执行的事故安全阈值,使高风险生物研究与其他危险行业使用的定量安全标准保持一致。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为针对大流行病原体制定更全面的安全框架提供信息,从而加强公共卫生准备和风险管理。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于概念分析,不涉及实证数据收集,这限制了证据的强度。然而,拟议的框架以现有安全标准为基础,被认为具有中等证据强度。

Complementing Biosafety Levels With Societal Safety Levels

Authors: Kevin M. Esvelt, Dana Gretton, Lennart J. Justen
Original abstract (English)
Biosafety level (BSL) classifications of biological agents have succeeded at minimizing occupational risk inside laboratories, but largely neglect the potential for onward transmission. As a result, many non-transmissible pathogens that could kill at most one individual per person exposed require greater containment than 1918 influenza, a virus that killed 50-100 million and might cause a new pandemic today. Laboratory workers are consenting professionals; the public is not. We recommend complementing BSLs with explicit thresholds for expected societal harm. Here we formalize Societal Safety Levels (SSLs) based on expected public fatalities per year, which we quantify from (i) estimated probability of laboratory release, (ii) likelihood of societal transmission, and (iii) consequence. SSLs would apply specifically to laboratory research with replication-competent potential pandemic pathogens, and would not restrict field surveillance, sequencing, or countermeasure development using non-replicating systems or pandemic-incapable agents. We find that applying the 0.1 expected deaths per facility-year standard common across multiple major-hazard industries to potential pandemic pathogen research would require marked safety improvements. Complementing BSLs with SSLs would establish enforceable accident-safety thresholds for pandemic-capable pathogens, bringing high-risk biological research into alignment with the quantitative safety standards that help other hazardous industries operate with public acceptance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Biosafety level (BSL) classifications of biological agents have succeeded at minimizing occupational risk inside laboratories, but largely neglect the potential for onward transmission. The study addresses the need for a framework that considers societal safety in addition to laboratory biosafety.
Design and population
The study is a conceptual and policy analysis proposing the use of Societal Safety Levels (SSLs) for laboratory research involving replication-competent potential pandemic pathogens. It does not involve empirical data collection or specific populations.
Core methods
SSLs are formalized based on expected public fatalities per year, calculated from (i) estimated probability of laboratory release, (ii) likelihood of societal transmission, and (iii) consequence. The methods focus on quantifying societal harm thresholds for pandemic-capable pathogens.
Main results
We find that applying the 0.1 expected deaths per facility-year standard common across multiple major-hazard industries to potential pandemic pathogen research would require marked safety improvements.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that complementing BSLs with SSLs would establish enforceable accident-safety thresholds for pandemic-capable pathogens, aligning high-risk biological research with quantitative safety standards used in other hazardous industries.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of a more comprehensive safety framework for pandemic-capable pathogens, enhancing public health preparedness and risk management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on conceptual analysis and does not involve empirical data collection, which limits the strength of the evidence. However, the proposed framework is grounded in existing safety standards and is considered to have moderate evidence strength.

Journal: medRxiv  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-02  |  DOI: 10.64898/2026.04.17.26351048  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window

通过全球 监测 数据的时间聚类定义流感流行区

Defining influenza epidemic zones through temporal clustering of global surveillance data
作者： Hassell, N., Marcenac, P., Bationo, C. S., Hirve, S., Tempia, S., Rolfes, M. A., Duca, L. M., Hammond, A., Wijesinghe, P. R., Heraud, J.-M.
摘要中文翻译简介:对流感流行通常发生的时间进行建模可以帮助各国优化 监测、安排临床和公共卫生干预措施并减轻流感负担。方法:我们使用了 2011-2024 年期间 180 个国家向全球流感 监测 和响应系统报告的流感病毒检测结果,不包括受 COVID-19 大流行影响的年份(2020-2023 年)。我们根据每个国家大多数流感检测发生的时间,按日历年(第 1-52 周)或轮班年(第 30-29 周)时间窗口分析数据。对于拥有足够数据的国家,我们计算了每个国家每周流感阳性检测的广义加性模型(GAM),以平滑和估算时间序列分布。根据这些 GAM,我们计算了每个国家每周的流感负担。使用集成共识 k 均值聚类对特定国家/地区的时间序列进行分组,从而减少聚类内时间序列之间的欧几里得距离。我们计算了特定于集群的 GAM 来估计平均季节性时间。没有足够数据的国家根据人口加权纬度与聚类平均纬度的距离分配到一个聚类。结果:我们在数据充足的 111 个国家中确定了 5 个集群或流行区。A区和B区的流感负担与北半球模式一致,大部分流感检测发生在11月至5月;而D区和E区则具有类似南半球的季节性时间特征,大部分流感负担发生在6月至11月(D)和5月至11月(E)。C区流感负担主要发生在9月-3月;大多数分配到该组的国家都位于热带地区。结论:流行区可以作为加强和优化流感 监测 的有用工具,以促进全球卫生决策(例如,在 疫苗 毒株组成讨论期间),并指导各国针对 季节性流感 流行病的准备工作,包括加强 监测 的时间安排和通报预防和控制工作的可用性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
对流感流行通常发生的时间进行建模可以帮助各国优化 监测、安排临床和公共卫生干预措施并减轻流感负担。
设计与对象
我们根据每个国家大多数流感检测发生的时间,按日历年(第 1-52 周)或轮班年(第 30-29 周)时间窗口分析数据。
核心方法
使用集成共识 k 均值聚类对特定国家/地区的时间序列进行分组,从而减少聚类内时间序列之间的欧几里得距离。
主要结果
我们在拥有充足数据的 111 个国家中确定了 5 个聚集性病例或流行区。
解释与创新
A区和B区的流感负担与北半球模式一致,大部分流感检测发生在11月至5月;而D区和E区则具有类似南半球的季节性时间特征,大部分流感负担发生在6月至11月(D)和5月至11月(E)。
科研与公卫意义
流行区可以作为加强和优化流感 监测 的有用工具,以促进全球卫生决策(例如,在 疫苗 菌株组成讨论期间),并指导各国针对 季节性流感 流行病的准备工作,包括加强 监测 的时间安排和通报预防和控制工作的可用性。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取仅基于抽象。该消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Defining influenza epidemic zones through temporal clustering of global surveillance data

Authors: Hassell, N., Marcenac, P., Bationo, C. S., Hirve, S., Tempia, S., Rolfes, M. A., Duca, L. M., Hammond, A., Wijesinghe, P. R., Heraud, J.-M.
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Modeling when influenza epidemics typically occur can help countries optimize surveillance, time clinical and public health interventions, and reduce the burden of influenza. Methods: We used influenza virus detections reported during 2011-2024 by 180 countries to the Global Influenza Surveillance and Response System, excluding years impacted by the COVID-19 pandemic (2020-2023). We analyzed data by calendar year (week 1-52) or shifted year (week 30-29) time windows, based on when most influenza detections occurred in each country. For countries with sufficient data, we computed generalized additive models (GAMs) of each country's weekly influenza-positive tests to smooth and impute time series distributions. From these GAMs, we calculated each country's weekly influenza burden. Country-specific time series were grouped using ensemble consensus k-means clustering, reducing the Euclidean distance between time series within clusters. We calculated cluster-specific GAMs to estimate average seasonal timing. Countries without sufficient data were assigned to a cluster based on population-weighted latitudinal distance to a cluster's mean latitude. Results: We identified five clusters, or epidemic zones, from 111 countries with sufficient data. The influenza burden in epidemic zones A and B was consistent with a northern hemisphere pattern, with most influenza detections occurring during November-May, while epidemic zones D and E were characterized by southern hemisphere-like seasonal timing, with most influenza burden occurring during June-November (D) and May-November (E). Epidemic zone C had most influenza burden occurring during September-March ; most countries assigned to this cluster were in the tropics. Conclusion: Epidemic zones may serve as a useful tool to strengthen and optimize influenza surveillance for global health decision-making (e.g., during vaccine strain composition discussions) and to guide country preparedness efforts for seasonal influenza epidemics, including timing enhanced surveillance and informing the availability of prevention and control efforts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Modeling when influenza epidemics typically occur can help countries optimize surveillance, time clinical and public health interventions, and reduce the burden of influenza.
Design and population
We analyzed data by calendar year (week 1-52) or shifted year (week 30-29) time windows, based on when most influenza detections occurred in each country.
Core methods
Country-specific time series were grouped using ensemble consensus k-means clustering, reducing the Euclidean distance between time series within clusters.
Main results
We identified five clusters, or epidemic zones, from 111 countries with sufficient data.
Interpretation and novelty
The influenza burden in epidemic zones A and B was consistent with a northern hemisphere pattern, with most influenza detections occurring during November-May, while epidemic zones D and E were characterized by southern hemisphere-like seasonal timing, with most influenza burden occurring during June-November (D) and May-November (E).
Scientific and public-health significance
Epidemic zones may serve as a useful tool to strengthen and optimize influenza surveillance for global health decision-making (e.g., during vaccine strain composition discussions) and to guide country preparedness efforts for seasonal influenza epidemics, including timing enhanced surveillance and informing the availability of prevention and control efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
This deterministic extraction is based on abstract only. The source does not explicitly report enough limitations for an independent appraisal, so confidence is low.

香港医护人员中几种灭活流感 疫苗 的免疫原性比较

Comparative immunogenicity of several inactivated influenza vaccines among healthcare workers in Hong Kong
作者： Lim, W. W., Touyon, L., Mak, L., Lau, Y. C., Cheng, S. M. S., Ip, D. K. M., Peiris, M., Cowling, B. J., Wong, S.-S.
摘要中文翻译我们比较了 2025/26 年季节期间香港成人医护人员中三种获得许可的鸡蛋灭活流感 疫苗 的免疫原性,包括 TetrAnflu(科兴四价)、Fluarix Tetra(葛兰素史克四价)和 Vaxigrip(赛诺菲三价)。从正在进行的 队列研究 中鉴定出年龄和性别匹配的受者(每组 n=30-40)以及 疫苗接种 前后配对的血清。针对卵繁殖的 2025/26 疫苗 参考菌株进行血凝抑制测定。三种 疫苗 诱导了大致可比的几何平均倍数上升、疫苗接种 后几何平均滴度、达到滴度 >=40 的比例以及针对 疫苗 菌株的血清转化率。这些真实数据支持医护人员之间 季节性流感 疫苗接种 计划的三种产品的互换性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在比较 2025/26 年季节期间香港医护人员中三种获得许可的鸡蛋灭活流感 疫苗 的免疫原性,以满足了解 疫苗 在该人群中有效性的需要。
设计与对象
从正在进行的 队列研究 中鉴定出年龄和性别匹配的受者(每组 n=30-40)以及 疫苗接种 前后配对的血清。
核心方法
针对卵繁殖的 2025/26 疫苗 参考菌株进行血凝抑制测定。
主要结果
三种 疫苗 诱导了大致可比的几何平均倍数上升、疫苗接种 后几何平均滴度、达到滴度 >=40 的比例以及针对 疫苗 菌株的血清转化率。
解释与创新
这些真实数据支持医护人员之间 季节性流感 疫苗接种 计划的三种产品的互换性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为针对医护人员的季节性 疫苗 计划中灭活流感 疫苗 的选择和互换性提供了具体的意义。
局限与证据强度
该研究由于依赖单个 队列研究 的真实世界数据而受到限制,但由于对三个 疫苗 的免疫原性参数进行了直接比较,因此证据充足。

Comparative immunogenicity of several inactivated influenza vaccines among healthcare workers in Hong Kong

Authors: Lim, W. W., Touyon, L., Mak, L., Lau, Y. C., Cheng, S. M. S., Ip, D. K. M., Peiris, M., Cowling, B. J., Wong, S.-S.
Original abstract (English)
We compared the immunogenicity of three licensed egg-based inactivated influenza vaccines, including TetrAnflu (Sinovac quadrivalent), Fluarix Tetra (GSK quadrivalent), and Vaxigrip (Sanofi trivalent), in adult healthcare workers in Hong Kong during the 2025/26 season. Age- and sex-matched recipients (n=30-40 per group) with paired pre- and post-vaccination sera were identified from an ongoing cohort. Hemagglutination-inhibition assays were performed against the egg-propagated 2025/26 vaccine reference strains. The three vaccines induced broadly comparable geometric mean fold-rises, post-vaccination geometric mean titers, proportions achieving titers >=40, and seroconversion rates against vaccine strains. These real-world data support the interchangeability of the three products for seasonal influenza vaccination programs among healthcare workers.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to compare the immunogenicity of three licensed egg-based inactivated influenza vaccines among healthcare workers in Hong Kong during the 2025/26 season to address the need for understanding vaccine effectiveness in this population.
Design and population
Age- and sex-matched recipients (n=30-40 per group) with paired pre- and post-vaccination sera were identified from an ongoing cohort.
Core methods
Hemagglutination-inhibition assays were performed against the egg-propagated 2025/26 vaccine reference strains.
Main results
The three vaccines induced broadly comparable geometric mean fold-rises, post-vaccination geometric mean titers, proportions achieving titers >=40, and seroconversion rates against vaccine strains.
Interpretation and novelty
These real-world data support the interchangeability of the three products for seasonal influenza vaccination programs among healthcare workers.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide specific implications for the selection and interchangeability of inactivated influenza vaccines in seasonal vaccination programs targeting healthcare workers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on real-world data from a single cohort, but the evidence is strong due to the direct comparison of immunogenicity parameters across the three vaccines.
